===== src/app/(app)/connections.tsx =====

import { Stack, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import { useMe, usePeopleList } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { followButton, useFollow } from "@/features/people/use-follow";
import { useRemoveFollower } from "@/features/people/use-requests";
import { colors, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Followers or Following of a profile. On your own followers: Remove. Elsewhere: Follow / Requested / Following. */
export default function Connections() {
  const { handle, list: listParam } = useLocalSearchParams<{ handle: string; list: string }>();
  const list = listParam === "following" ? "following" : "followers";
  const people = usePeopleList(handle, list);
  const me = useMe();
  const follow = useFollow();
  const remove = useRemoveFollower();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const ownFollowers = list === "followers" && me.data?.user.handle === handle;
  const rows = people.data?.people ?? [];
  const isPrivate = people.error instanceof ApiError && people.error.code === "private_account";

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await people.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: list === "followers" ? "Followers" : "Following" }} />
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        {people.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={5} />
        ) : isPrivate ? (
          <View style={styles.private}>
            <Icon name="lock.fill" color="textSecondary" size={size.icon} />
            <Text variant="headline">This account is private</Text>
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              Follow @{handle} to see who they follow and who follows them.
            </Text>
          </View>
        ) : people.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(people.error, "Couldn't load this list.")} onRetry={() => void people.refetch()} retrying={people.isFetching} />
        ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            {list === "followers" ? "No followers yet." : "Not following anyone yet."}
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup>
            {rows.map((p) => {
              if (p.isSelf) return <PersonRow key={p.handle} person={p} subtitle="You" />;
              if (ownFollowers) {
                const label = `Remove @${p.handle} as a follower`;
                return (
                  <PersonRow
                    key={p.handle}
                    person={p}
                    actions={[{ label, onPress: () => remove.confirm(p.handle) }]}
                    trailing={<Button title="Remove" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={() => remove.confirm(p.handle)} accessibilityLabel={label} />}
                  />
                );
              }
              const b = followButton(p.follow, p.handle);
              const toggle = () => follow.toggle(p.handle, p.follow);
              return (
                <PersonRow
                  key={p.handle}
                  person={p}
                  actions={[{ label: b.label, onPress: toggle }]}
                  trailing={
                    <Button
                      title={b.title}
                      variant={b.variant}
                      compact
                      block={false}
                      onPress={toggle}
                      accessibilityLabel={b.label}
                      accessibilityState={{ busy: follow.isPendingFor(p.handle) }}
                    />
                  }
                />
              );
            })}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  private: { gap: space.xs, alignItems: "flex-start" },
});


===== src/app/(app)/edit-profile.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { ActionSheetIOS, Alert, Pressable, StyleSheet, Switch, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { AvatarResponse, type ProfileSettingsResponse } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk, useProfileSettings, useUserProfile } from "@/api/queries";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { BIO_MAX, goesPublic, goingPublicAlert, NAME_MAX, privacyNote, profileChanges, profileProblem, shouldOfferApproveAll, type ProfileValues } from "@/features/people/edit-profile";
import { confirmApproveAll, useApproveAll } from "@/features/people/use-requests";
import { pickFromLibrary } from "@/features/photos/pick";
import { prepareImage } from "@/features/photos/prepare";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { useConfirmDiscard } from "@/lib/use-confirm-discard";
import { colors, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Profile photo, display name, bio (150) and the Private account toggle. */
export default function EditProfile() {
  const settings = useProfileSettings();
  const data = settings.data;
  if (!data)
    return (
      <FormScreen>
        {settings.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(settings.error, "Couldn't load your profile.")} onRetry={() => void settings.refetch()} retrying={settings.isFetching} />
        ) : (
          <SkeletonRows count={4} />
        )}
      </FormScreen>
    );
  return <EditForm data={data} />;
}

function EditForm({ data }: { data: ProfileSettingsResponse }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const initial: ProfileValues = { displayName: data.profile.displayName, bio: data.profile.bio, isPrivate: data.profile.isPrivate };
  const [values, setValues] = useState(initial);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const approveAll = useApproveAll();
  const profile = useUserProfile(data.profile.handle);
  const change = profileChanges(initial, values);
  const dirty = Object.keys(change).length > 0;
  const problem = profileProblem(values);
  useConfirmDiscard(dirty && !saved);

  const refreshProfile = () => {
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.me, exact: true });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.profileSettings });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.user(data.profile.handle) });
  };

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.me.updateProfile(change),
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      haptics.success();
      setSaved(true);
      refreshProfile();
      if (shouldOfferApproveAll(change, res.pendingRequests)) {
        // Going public doesn't approve anyone by itself.
        confirmApproveAll(res.pendingRequests, () => approveAll.mutate(), () => router.back());
        return;
      }
      requestAnimationFrame(() => router.back());
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const photo = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (action: "choose" | "remove") => {
      if (action === "remove") return api.me.removeAvatar();
      const picked = await pickFromLibrary();
      if (!picked) return null;
      const prepared = await prepareImage(picked.uri);
      const form = new FormData();
      // React Native's FormData takes a file descriptor object for local files.
      form.append("photo", { uri: prepared.uri, name: "avatar.jpg", type: "image/jpeg" } as unknown as Blob);
      return api.upload("/api/me/avatar", AvatarResponse, form);
    },
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      if (!res) return;
      haptics.success();
      toast.show(res.avatarUrl ? "Photo updated" : "Photo removed", "neutral");
      refreshProfile();
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const avatarUrl = photo.data !== undefined && photo.data !== null ? photo.data.avatarUrl : data.profile.avatarUrl;

  const choosePhoto = () => {
    const options = avatarUrl ? ["Choose photo", "Remove photo", "Cancel"] : ["Choose photo", "Cancel"];
    ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
      { options, cancelButtonIndex: options.length - 1, destructiveButtonIndex: avatarUrl ? 1 : undefined, tintColor: colors.accent, userInterfaceStyle: "dark" },
      (i) => {
        if (i === 0) photo.mutate("choose");
        if (avatarUrl && i === 1) photo.mutate("remove");
      },
    );
  };

  /** Switching private to public changes what strangers can see, so it needs an explicit yes first. */
  const onSave = () => {
    if (!goesPublic(initial, change)) return save.mutate();
    const counts = profile.data?.access === "full" ? profile.data.user.counts : undefined;
    const a = goingPublicAlert(counts);
    Alert.alert(a.title, a.message, [
      { text: "Keep private", style: "cancel" },
      { text: a.confirm, style: "destructive", onPress: () => save.mutate() },
    ]);
  };

  const set = (k: "displayName" | "bio") => (t: string) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, [k]: t }));

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerRight: () => <HeaderButton label="Save" bold onPress={onSave} disabled={!dirty || !!problem || save.isPending} />,
        }}
      />
      <Pressable
        onPress={choosePhoto}
        disabled={photo.isPending}
        accessibilityRole="button"
        accessibilityLabel={avatarUrl ? "Change profile photo" : "Add profile photo"}
        accessibilityState={{ busy: photo.isPending }}
        style={styles.photo}
      >
        <Avatar name={values.displayName} uri={avatarUrl} size={size.avatarLarge} variant="title" />
        <Text variant="callout" color="accent" weight="semibold">
          {photo.isPending ? "Uploading…" : avatarUrl ? "Change photo" : "Add photo"}
        </Text>
      </Pressable>
      {photo.error && <InlineError message={errorText(photo.error, "Couldn't update your photo. Try again.")} />}

      <FieldGroup footer={`${values.bio.trim().length}/${BIO_MAX}`} errors={[problem && dirty ? problem : null]}>
        <Field label="Name" value={values.displayName} onChangeText={set("displayName")} maxLength={NAME_MAX} autoCapitalize="words" textContentType="name" returnKeyType="next" />
        <Field
          label="Bio"
          value={values.bio}
          onChangeText={set("bio")}
          maxLength={BIO_MAX}
          placeholder="A line about you"
          multiline
          accessibilityHint={`Up to ${BIO_MAX} characters`}
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <View style={styles.privateWrap}>
        <View style={styles.privateRow}>
          <Text variant="body" style={styles.flex}>
            Private account
          </Text>
          <Switch
            value={values.isPrivate}
            onValueChange={(isPrivate) => setValues((v) => ({ ...v, isPrivate }))}
            trackColor={{ true: colors.accent, false: colors.elevated }}
            accessibilityLabel="Private account"
            accessibilityHint={privacyNote(values.isPrivate)}
          />
        </View>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" style={styles.note}>
          {privacyNote(values.isPrivate)}
        </Text>
      </View>

      {save.error && <InlineError message={errorText(save.error, "Couldn't save. Check the fields and try again.")} />}
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  photo: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs, paddingTop: space.md },
  privateWrap: { gap: space.xs },
  privateRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    minHeight: size.fieldHeight,
    paddingHorizontal: space.md,
    backgroundColor: colors.surface,
    borderRadius: radius.card,
  },
  flex: { flex: 1 },
  note: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
});


===== src/app/(app)/follow-requests.tsx =====

import { Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useFollowRequests } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { confirmApproveAll, useApproveAll, useDecideRequest } from "@/features/people/use-requests";
import { formatAge } from "@/lib/format";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** People asking to follow your private account: Approve or Decline each, or approve them all. */
export default function FollowRequests() {
  const requests = useFollowRequests();
  const decide = useDecideRequest();
  const approveAll = useApproveAll();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const rows = requests.data?.requests ?? [];

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await requests.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          headerRight:
            rows.length > 1
              ? () => <HeaderButton label="Approve all" disabled={approveAll.isPending} onPress={() => confirmApproveAll(rows.length, () => approveAll.mutate())} />
              : undefined,
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        {requests.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={4} />
        ) : requests.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(requests.error, "Couldn't load requests.")} onRetry={() => void requests.refetch()} retrying={requests.isFetching} />
        ) : rows.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            No requests right now.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup footer="Approved people see your stamps and places.">
            {rows.map(({ person, requestedAt }) => {
              const approve = () => decide.mutate({ handle: person.handle, approve: true });
              const decline = () => decide.mutate({ handle: person.handle, approve: false });
              return (
                <PersonRow
                  key={person.handle}
                  person={person}
                  subtitle={formatAge(requestedAt)}
                  actions={[
                    { label: `Approve @${person.handle}`, onPress: approve },
                    { label: `Decline @${person.handle}`, onPress: decline },
                  ]}
                  trailing={
                    <View style={styles.buttons}>
                      <Button title="Approve" compact block={false} onPress={approve} accessibilityLabel={`Approve @${person.handle}`} />
                      <Button title="Decline" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={decline} accessibilityLabel={`Decline @${person.handle}`} />
                    </View>
                  }
                />
              );
            })}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  buttons: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/app/(app)/location-permission.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Linking, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { locationStore, useLocationState } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Pre-permission screen: one line on why, then the system prompt. Denied gets a path to Settings. */
export default function LocationPermission() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { permission } = useLocationState();
  const [asking, setAsking] = useState(false);
  const denied = permission === "denied";

  const ask = async () => {
    setAsking(true);
    const result = await locationStore.request().finally(() => setAsking(false));
    if (result === "granted") router.back();
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.md }]}>
      <View style={styles.body}>
        <View style={styles.badge}>
          <Icon name="location.fill" color="accent" size={size.iconLarge} />
        </View>
        <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
          {denied ? "Location is off" : "Find places near you"}
        </Text>
        <Text color="textSecondary">
          {denied
            ? "Nostos can't see your location. Turn it on in Settings to find the places near you and to stamp them."
            : "Nostos uses your location while you're using the app to find the places near you and to confirm you're there when you stamp one. Your position isn't shown to anyone."}
        </Text>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {denied ? (
          <Button title="Open Settings" onPress={() => void Linking.openSettings()} />
        ) : (
          <Button title="Continue" onPress={() => void ask()} loading={asking} />
        )}
        <Button title="Not now" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.back()} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.surface, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.xxxl, justifyContent: "space-between" },
  body: { gap: space.sm },
  badge: {
    width: size.avatarLarge,
    height: size.avatarLarge,
    borderRadius: radius.largeCard,
    backgroundColor: colors.elevated,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    marginBottom: space.xs,
  },
  actions: { gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/app/(app)/moderation.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText, mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { TargetType, ModQueueItem } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useModHidden, useModLog, useModQueue } from "@/api/queries";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SegmentedControl } from "@/components/ui/segmented-control";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { toast, ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { actionWord, reputationText } from "@/features/moderation/format";
import { formatAge, plural } from "@/lib/format";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

type Tab = "queue" | "hidden" | "log";
type Target = { targetType: TargetType; targetId: string; author: string; venue: string | null; autoHidden?: boolean };
type Action = "HIDE" | "DISMISS" | "RESTORE" | "SUSPEND" | "UNSUSPEND";

const NOUN: Record<TargetType, string> = { STAMP: "Stamp", USER: "Account" };
/** The log keeps the database's names, including content types older entries may carry. */
const LOG_NOUN: Record<string, string> = { PHOTO: "Stamp", COMMENT: "Comment", REPORT: "Report", USER: "Account" };
const VERB: Record<Action, { title: string; done: string; destructive: boolean }> = {
  HIDE: { title: "Hide", done: "Hidden.", destructive: true },
  DISMISS: { title: "Dismiss flags", done: "Flags dismissed.", destructive: false },
  RESTORE: { title: "Restore", done: "Restored.", destructive: false },
  SUSPEND: { title: "Suspend account", done: "Account suspended.", destructive: true },
  UNSUSPEND: { title: "Unsuspend", done: "Account unsuspended.", destructive: false },
};

/** Moderators: the flag queue grouped by item, hidden content to restore, and the log. */
export default function Moderation() {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("queue");
  const queue = useModQueue();
  const hidden = useModHidden(tab === "hidden");
  const log = useModLog(tab === "log");
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);

  const act = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { targetType: TargetType; targetId: string; action: Action; note?: string }) => api.mod.act(v),
    onSuccess: (_r, v) => {
      haptics.success();
      toast.show(VERB[v.action].done);
      for (const key of [["mod"], ["feed"], ["user"], ["stamp"]]) void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: key });
    },
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't do that. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });

  /** Confirm, naming the item, with an optional note (kept in the moderation log). */
  const confirm = (t: Target, action: Action) => {
    const v = VERB[action];
    const what = t.targetType === "USER" ? t.author : `${NOUN[t.targetType].toLowerCase()} by ${t.author}${t.venue ? ` at ${t.venue}` : ""}`;
    const effect = action === "DISMISS" && t.autoHidden ? " It will be visible again." : "";
    const { targetType, targetId } = t;
    Alert.prompt(
      `${v.title}: ${what}?`,
      `Add a note for the log (optional).${effect}`,
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        { text: v.title, style: v.destructive ? "destructive" : "default", onPress: (note?: string) => act.mutate({ targetType, targetId, action, note: note?.trim() || undefined }) },
      ],
      "plain-text",
      "",
      "default",
    );
  };

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    const q = tab === "queue" ? queue : tab === "hidden" ? hidden : log;
    await q.refetch().finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        <SegmentedControl
          segments={[
            { key: "queue", label: queue.data ? `Queue (${queue.data.items.length})` : "Queue" },
            { key: "hidden", label: "Hidden" },
            { key: "log", label: "Log" },
          ]}
          value={tab}
          onChange={setTab}
          accessibilityLabel="Moderation sections"
        />

        {tab === "queue" &&
          (queue.isPending ? (
            <SkeletonRows count={4} />
          ) : queue.isError ? (
            <InlineError message={errorText(queue.error, "Couldn't load the queue.")} onRetry={() => void queue.refetch()} retrying={queue.isFetching} />
          ) : queue.data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              Nothing to review. The queue is clear.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            queue.data.items.map((item) => (
              <QueueCard
                key={`${item.targetType}:${item.targetId}`}
                item={item}
                busy={act.isPending && act.variables?.targetId === item.targetId}
                onAct={(a) =>
                  confirm(
                    {
                      targetType: item.targetType,
                      targetId: item.targetId,
                      author: item.content?.author ?? "unknown",
                      venue: item.venue?.name ?? null,
                      autoHidden: !!item.content?.hiddenAt && item.history.some((h) => h.action === "AUTO_HIDE"),
                    },
                    a,
                  )
                }
              />
            ))
          ))}

        {tab === "hidden" &&
          (hidden.isPending ? (
            <SkeletonRows count={4} />
          ) : hidden.isError ? (
            <InlineError message={errorText(hidden.error, "Couldn't load hidden items.")} onRetry={() => void hidden.refetch()} retrying={hidden.isFetching} />
          ) : hidden.data.items.length === 0 ? (
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              Nothing is hidden or suspended.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <ListGroup>
              {hidden.data.items.map((h) => (
                <ListRow
                  key={`${h.targetType}:${h.targetId}`}
                  leading={h.url ? <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(h.url) }} style={styles.thumb} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel={`Hidden photo by ${h.author}`} /> : undefined}
                  title={h.preview || NOUN[h.targetType]}
                  subtitle={`${NOUN[h.targetType]} by ${h.author}${h.venue ? ` at ${h.venue.name}` : ""} · ${formatAge(h.hiddenAt)}`}
                  trailing={
                    <Button
                      title={h.targetType === "USER" ? "Unsuspend" : "Restore"}
                      variant="plain"
                      compact
                      block={false}
                      onPress={() => confirm({ targetType: h.targetType, targetId: h.targetId, author: h.author, venue: h.venue?.name ?? null }, h.targetType === "USER" ? "UNSUSPEND" : "RESTORE")}
                      accessibilityLabel={`${h.targetType === "USER" ? "Unsuspend" : "Restore"} ${NOUN[h.targetType].toLowerCase()} by ${h.author}`}
                    />
                  }
                />
              ))}
            </ListGroup>
          ))}

        {tab === "log" &&
          (log.isPending ? (
            <SkeletonRows count={6} />
          ) : log.isError ? (
            <InlineError message={errorText(log.error, "Couldn't load the log.")} onRetry={() => void log.refetch()} retrying={log.isFetching} />
          ) : log.data.events.length === 0 ? (
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              No moderation actions yet.
            </Text>
          ) : (
            <ListGroup footer="The log is permanent: entries are never edited or removed.">
              {log.data.events.map((e) => (
                <ListRow
                  key={e.id}
                  title={`${actionWord(e.action)} \u00B7 ${LOG_NOUN[e.targetType] ?? e.targetType}`}
                  subtitle={`${e.by} \u00B7 ${formatAge(e.createdAt)}${e.note ? ` \u00B7 \u201C${e.note}\u201D` : ""}`}
                />
              ))}
            </ListGroup>
          ))}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

function QueueCard({ item, busy, onAct }: { item: ModQueueItem; busy: boolean; onAct: (a: Action) => void }) {
  const c = item.content;
  const isUser = item.targetType === "USER";
  const hidden = !!c?.hiddenAt;
  // Flags are listed above; the history shows only what moderators (or auto-hide) did.
  const history = item.history.filter((h) => h.action !== "FLAGGED");
  const who = `${NOUN[item.targetType].toLowerCase()} by ${c?.author ?? "unknown"}`;
  return (
    <View style={styles.card}>
      <View style={styles.cardHead}>
        <Badge label={NOUN[item.targetType]} />
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" style={styles.flex} numberOfLines={1}>
          {item.venue ? item.venue.name : "Account report"} · {plural(item.flags.length, "flag")}
        </Text>
        {c?.hiddenAt && <Badge label={isUser ? "Suspended" : "Hidden"} />}
      </View>

      {c ? (
        <View style={styles.preview}>
          {c.url && <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(c.url) }} style={styles.photo} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel="Flagged stamp" />}
          {!!c.preview && <Text variant="body">{c.preview}</Text>}
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            {c.author} · {reputationText(c.authorRep)} · {formatAge(c.createdAt)}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
          The content is no longer available.
        </Text>
      )}

      <View style={styles.flags}>
        {item.flags.map((f) => (
          <View key={f.id} style={styles.flag}>
            <Text variant="subhead">
              {f.category === "UNDERAGE" || f.category === "STALKING" ? "Urgent: " : ""}&ldquo;{f.reason}&rdquo;
            </Text>
            <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
              {f.by}
              {f.byRole !== "USER" ? ` · ${f.byRole.toLowerCase()}` : ""} · {formatAge(f.createdAt)}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>

      {history.length > 0 && (
        <View style={styles.flag}>
          {history.map((h, i) => (
            <Text key={i} variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
              {actionWord(h.action)} by {h.by} · {formatAge(h.at)}
              {h.note ? ` \u00B7 \u201C${h.note}\u201D` : ""}
            </Text>
          ))}
        </View>
      )}

      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {isUser ? (
          hidden ? (
            <Button title="Unsuspend" variant="secondary" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("UNSUSPEND")} accessibilityLabel={`Unsuspend ${c?.author}`} />
          ) : (
            <Button title="Suspend" variant="destructive" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("SUSPEND")} accessibilityLabel={`Suspend ${c?.author}`} />
          )
        ) : (
          <Button title={hidden ? "Keep hidden" : "Hide"} variant="destructive" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("HIDE")} accessibilityLabel={`${hidden ? "Keep hidden" : "Hide"} ${who}`} />
        )}
        <Button title="Dismiss" variant="secondary" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("DISMISS")} accessibilityLabel={`Dismiss flags on ${who}`} />
        {!isUser && hidden && <Button title="Restore" variant="plain" compact block={false} disabled={busy} onPress={() => onAct("RESTORE")} accessibilityLabel={`Restore ${who}`} />}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.xs, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.md },
  card: { gap: space.sm, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  cardHead: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs },
  preview: { gap: space.xs },
  photo: { height: size.heroHeight, borderRadius: radius.chip, backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  flags: { gap: space.xs, paddingTop: space.xs, borderTopWidth: hairlineWidth, borderTopColor: colors.hairline },
  flag: { gap: space.xxs },
  actions: { flexDirection: "row", gap: space.xs, flexWrap: "wrap" },
  thumb: { width: size.avatar, height: size.avatar, borderRadius: radius.chip },
  flex: { flex: 1 },
});


===== src/app/(app)/people.tsx =====

import { Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useUserSearch } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { PersonRow } from "@/features/people/person-row";
import { followButton, useFollow } from "@/features/people/use-follow";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** Find people by name or handle and follow them. Native search bar in the navigation bar. */
export default function People() {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const search = useUserSearch(query);
  const follow = useFollow();

  // Search as you type, after a short pause.
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setQuery(input.trim().replace(/^@/, "")), 250);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [input]);

  const users = search.data?.users ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          headerSearchBarOptions: {
            placeholder: "Search by name or handle",
            autoCapitalize: "none",
            autoFocus: true,
            hideWhenScrolling: false,
            barTintColor: colors.surface,
            tintColor: colors.accent,
            textColor: colors.textPrimary,
            onChangeText: (e) => setInput(e.nativeEvent.text),
          },
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" keyboardDismissMode="on-drag" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {query.length < 2 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            Type at least two letters.
          </Text>
        ) : search.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={3} />
        ) : search.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(search.error, "Couldn't search right now.")} onRetry={() => void search.refetch()} retrying={search.isFetching} />
        ) : users.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            No one matches {"“"}
            {query}
            {"”"}.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup>
            {users.map((u) => {
              const b = followButton(u.follow, u.handle);
              const toggle = () => follow.toggle(u.handle, u.follow);
              return (
                <PersonRow
                  key={u.handle}
                  person={u}
                  subtitle={u.isSelf ? "You" : undefined}
                  actions={u.isSelf ? [] : [{ label: b.label, onPress: toggle }]}
                  trailing={
                    u.isSelf ? undefined : <Button
                      title={b.title}
                      variant={b.variant}
                      compact
                      block={false}
                      onPress={toggle}
                      accessibilityLabel={b.label}
                      accessibilityState={{ busy: follow.isPendingFor(u.handle) }}
                    />
                  }
                />
              );
            })}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
});


===== src/app/(app)/settings/blocked.tsx =====

import { ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useBlocks } from "@/api/queries";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { useBlock } from "@/features/moderation/content-actions";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

export default function Blocked() {
  const blocks = useBlocks();
  const block = useBlock();
  const list = blocks.data?.blocks ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {blocks.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={3} />
        ) : blocks.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(blocks.error, "Couldn't load blocked users.")} onRetry={() => void blocks.refetch()} retrying={blocks.isFetching} />
        ) : list.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            You haven&apos;t blocked anyone.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup footer="Blocked people can't see your stamps or profile, and you can't see theirs.">
            {list.map((b) => (
              <ListRow
                key={b.handle}
                leading={<Avatar name={b.handle} />}
                title={`@${b.handle}`}
                trailing={
                  <Button
                    title="Unblock"
                    variant="secondary"
                    compact
                    block={false}
                    loading={block.isPending && block.variables?.handle === b.handle}
                    onPress={() => block.mutate({ handle: b.handle, block: false })}
                    accessibilityLabel={`Unblock @${b.handle}`}
                  />
                }
              />
            ))}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.md },
});


===== src/app/(app)/settings/delete-account.tsx =====

import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Alert, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import { farewellNotice, useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

const WHAT_HAPPENS = [
  "Your name, handle, email and password are erased.",
  "Your stamps are removed from every passport, and so are your follows and likes.",
  "You\u2019re signed out on every device. This can\u2019t be undone.",
];

const FAREWELL = "Your account is deleted.";

/** Guideline 5.1.1(v): delete the account from inside the app. Two taps from Profile to the button. */
export default function DeleteAccount() {
  const { forget } = useAuth();
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  const del = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      try {
        return await api.me.deleteAccount(password);
      } catch (e) {
        // A retry after a timeout that actually succeeded: the token is already dead, so it's done.
        if (e instanceof ApiError && e.code === "invalid_token") {
          farewellNotice.set(FAREWELL);
          return { deleted: true as const };
        }
        throw e;
      }
    },
    onMutate: () => farewellNotice.set(FAREWELL),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      // The account is gone; the token already stopped working. Sign out locally only.
      await forget();
    },
    onError: () => {
      farewellNotice.take();
      haptics.warning();
    },
  });

  const confirm = () => {
    if (!password || del.isPending) return;
    Alert.alert("Delete your account?", "This can't be undone.", [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      { text: "Delete", style: "destructive", onPress: () => del.mutate() },
    ]);
  };

  const wrongPassword = del.error instanceof ApiError && del.error.code === "wrong_password";

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <View style={styles.warning}>
        <Icon name="exclamationmark.triangle.fill" color="danger" size={size.iconLarge} />
        <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
          Delete your account
        </Text>
        {WHAT_HAPPENS.map((line) => (
          <View key={line} style={styles.bullet}>
            <View style={styles.dot} />
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" style={styles.flex}>
              {line}
            </Text>
          </View>
        ))}
      </View>

      <FieldGroup footer="Enter your password to confirm." errors={[wrongPassword ? "That password isn't right." : null]}>
        <Field
          label="Password"
          placeholder="Required"
          value={password}
          invalid={wrongPassword}
          onChangeText={(v) => {
            setPassword(v);
            if (del.error) del.reset();
          }}
          secureTextEntry
          autoComplete="current-password"
          textContentType="password"
          returnKeyType="done"
          onSubmitEditing={confirm}
          testID="delete-password"
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      {del.error && !wrongPassword && <InlineError message={errorText(del.error, "Couldn't delete your account. Try again.")} />}
      <Button title="Delete account" variant="destructive" onPress={confirm} disabled={!password} loading={del.isPending} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  warning: { gap: space.sm, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  bullet: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "flex-start", gap: space.sm },
  dot: { width: space.xs, height: space.xs, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.textTertiary, marginTop: space.xs },
  flex: { flex: 1 },
});


===== src/app/(app)/settings/index.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { Alert, RefreshControl, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useState } from "react";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useFollowing, useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { VerifiedBadge } from "@/components/ui/verified-badge";
import { LEGAL, openWebPage } from "@/lib/links";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** Settings: who I follow, the moderation entry for staff, blocked users, account and legal. */
export default function Settings() {
  const me = useMe();
  const following = useFollowing();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const user = me.data?.user;
  const staff = user?.role === "MODERATOR" || user?.role === "ADMIN";

  const refresh = async () => {
    setRefreshing(true);
    await Promise.all([me.refetch(), following.refetch()]).finally(() => setRefreshing(false));
  };

  const confirmSignOut = () =>
    Alert.alert("Sign out?", undefined, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      { text: "Sign out", style: "destructive", onPress: () => void signOut() },
    ]);

  const list = following.data?.following ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <ScrollView
        contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic"
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        refreshControl={<RefreshControl refreshing={refreshing} onRefresh={() => void refresh()} tintColor={colors.textSecondary} />}
      >
        {staff && (
          <ListGroup>
            <ListRow icon="shield.lefthalf.filled" iconColor="accent" title="Moderation" chevron onPress={() => router.push("/moderation")} />
          </ListGroup>
        )}

        <ListGroup header="Following">
          {following.isPending ? (
            <View style={styles.pad}>
              <Skeleton width="60%" />
            </View>
          ) : following.isError ? (
            <View style={styles.pad}>
              <InlineError message={errorText(following.error, "Couldn't load who you follow.")} onRetry={() => void following.refetch()} />
            </View>
          ) : list.length === 0 ? (
            <View style={styles.empty}>
              <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
                You&apos;re not following anyone yet.
              </Text>
              <Button title="Find people" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/people")} />
            </View>
          ) : (
            list.map((f) => (
              <ListRow
                key={f.handle}
                leading={<Avatar name={f.displayName} uri={f.avatarUrl} />}
                title={f.displayName}
                titleAccessory={f.verified ? <VerifiedBadge accessible={false} /> : undefined}
                accessibilityLabel={[f.displayName, `@${f.handle}`, f.verified ? "verified" : null].filter(Boolean).join(", ")}
                subtitle={`@${f.handle}`}
                chevron
                onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/user/[handle]", params: { handle: f.handle } })}
              />
            ))
          )}
          {list.length > 0 && <ListRow icon="person.badge.plus" iconColor="accent" title="Find people" titleColor="accent" onPress={() => router.push("/people")} />}
        </ListGroup>

        <ListGroup header="Settings">
          <ListRow icon="hand.raised.fill" title="Blocked users" chevron onPress={() => router.push("/settings/blocked")} />
          <ListRow icon="lock.fill" title="Privacy policy" external onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.privacy)} />
          <ListRow icon="doc.text.fill" title="Terms" external onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.terms)} />
          <ListRow icon="questionmark.circle.fill" title="Support" external onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.support)} />
        </ListGroup>

        <ListGroup>
          <ListRow icon="rectangle.portrait.and.arrow.right" title="Sign out" onPress={confirmSignOut} />
          <ListRow
            icon="trash.fill"
            iconColor="danger"
            title="Delete account"
            titleColor="danger"
            onPress={() => router.push("/settings/delete-account")}
            accessibilityHint="Opens the account deletion screen"
          />
        </ListGroup>

        {user && (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" style={styles.since}>
            Member since {new Date(user.memberSince).toLocaleDateString("en-US", { month: "long", year: "numeric" })}
          </Text>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.xl },
  pad: { padding: space.md },
  empty: { padding: space.md, gap: space.xs, alignItems: "flex-start" },
  since: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/[id].tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router, Stack, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { Alert, Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, ApiError, errorText, mediaUrl } from "@/api";
import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { qk, useStamp } from "@/api/queries";
import { Avatar } from "@/components/ui/avatar";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { toast, ToastHost } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { VerifiedBadge } from "@/components/ui/verified-badge";
import { a11yActions, showActions, useStampActions, type ContentAction } from "@/features/moderation/content-actions";
import { dropStamp } from "@/features/stamps/cache";
import { LikeButton } from "@/features/stamps/like-button";
import { SongRow } from "@/features/stamps/song-row";
import { stampLabel } from "@/features/stamps/stamp-card";
import { useLike } from "@/features/stamps/use-like";
import { formatAge } from "@/lib/format";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { aspect, colors, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** A stamp: photo, caption, song, place, author, likes. Yours: Edit and Delete from profile. Others': Report and Block. */
export default function StampDetail() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const stamp = useStamp(id);
  const s = stamp.data?.stamp;
  const actions = useActions(s);
  const gone = stamp.error instanceof ApiError && (stamp.error.status === 404 || stamp.error.status === 403);

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: s ? s.place.name : "Stamp",
          headerRight: () =>
            s && actions.length > 0 ? (
              <Pressable onPress={() => showActions(actions)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="More options" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.headerButton}>
                <Icon name="ellipsis.circle" color="accent" size={size.icon} />
              </Pressable>
            ) : null,
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {s ? (
          <Detail stamp={s} actions={actions} />
        ) : gone ? (
          <View style={styles.pad}>
            <Text variant="title2">This stamp isn&apos;t available</Text>
            <Text color="textSecondary">It may have been removed, or the account may be private.</Text>
            <Button title="Close" variant="secondary" compact block={false} onPress={() => router.back()} />
          </View>
        ) : stamp.isError ? (
          <View style={styles.pad}>
            <InlineError message={errorText(stamp.error, "Couldn't load this stamp.")} onRetry={() => void stamp.refetch()} retrying={stamp.isFetching} />
          </View>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Skeleton width="100%" height={size.postPhotoSkeleton} />
            <View style={styles.pad}>
              <Skeleton width="40%" />
              <Skeleton width="70%" />
            </View>
          </>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
      <ToastHost bottom={space.xxl} />
    </View>
  );
}

function Detail({ stamp, actions }: { stamp: StampView; actions: ContentAction[] }) {
  const like = useLike();
  return (
    <>
      {stamp.photoUrl && (
        <View accessible accessibilityRole="image" accessibilityLabel={stampLabel(stamp)} {...a11yActions(actions)}>
          <Image source={{ uri: mediaUrl(stamp.photoUrl), cacheKey: stamp.id }} style={styles.photo} contentFit="cover" transition={150} accessible={false} />
        </View>
      )}
      <View style={styles.pad}>
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: "/user/[handle]", params: { handle: stamp.author.handle } })}
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel={`@${stamp.author.handle}${stamp.author.verified ? ", verified" : ""}`}
          accessibilityHint="Opens their passport"
          style={styles.who}
        >
          <Avatar name={stamp.author.displayName} uri={stamp.author.avatarUrl} />
          <View style={styles.names}>
            <View style={styles.handleRow}>
              <Text variant="headline">{stamp.author.displayName}</Text>
              {stamp.author.verified && <VerifiedBadge accessible={false} />}
            </View>
            <Text variant="subhead" color="textSecondary">
              @{stamp.author.handle} · at {stamp.place.name}
            </Text>
          </View>
        </Pressable>

        {stamp.caption !== "" && <Text variant="body">{stamp.caption}</Text>}
        {stamp.song && <SongRow song={stamp.song} />}

        <View style={styles.footer}>
          <LikeButton likes={stamp.likes} disabled={stamp.mine} onPress={() => like.toggle(stamp.id, stamp.likes)} />
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            {formatAge(stamp.createdAt)}
            {stamp.editedAt ? " · Edited" : ""}
          </Text>
        </View>
        {stamp.mine && (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
            Only people who can see your passport can see this stamp.
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>
    </>
  );
}

/** Yours: Edit and Delete from profile (hides it for good). Others': Report and Block. */
function useActions(stamp: StampView | undefined): ContentAction[] {
  const build = useStampActions();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const remove = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => api.stamps.remove(id),
    onSuccess: (_r, id) => {
      haptics.success();
      toast.show("Deleted from your profile.");
      dropStamp(queryClient, id);
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.feed });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user"] });
      router.back();
    },
    onError: (e) => {
      haptics.warning();
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't delete that. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });
  if (!stamp) return [];
  const extra: ContentAction[] = stamp.mine
    ? [
        { label: "Edit", onPress: () => router.push({ pathname: "/stamp/edit", params: { id: stamp.id } }) },
        {
          label: "Delete from profile",
          destructive: true,
          onPress: () =>
            Alert.alert("Delete from your profile?", "It's removed from your passport and everyone's feed for good.", [
              { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
              { text: "Delete", style: "destructive", onPress: () => remove.mutate(stamp.id) },
            ]),
        },
      ]
    : [];
  return build({ id: stamp.id, authorHandle: stamp.author.handle, mine: stamp.mine, extra });
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingBottom: space.xxxl },
  photo: { width: "100%", aspectRatio: aspect.post, backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  pad: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, gap: space.md },
  who: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm, minHeight: size.hitTarget },
  names: { flex: 1, gap: space.xxs },
  handleRow: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xxs },
  footer: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.md },
  headerButton: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "flex-end", justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/edit.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router, Stack, useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { Song, StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useStamp } from "@/api/queries";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { CAPTION_MAX, stampChanges } from "@/features/stamps/composer";
import { patchStamp } from "@/features/stamps/cache";
import { songDraft } from "@/features/stamps/song-draft";
import { SongRow } from "@/features/stamps/song-row";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { useConfirmDiscard } from "@/lib/use-confirm-discard";
import { size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Edit a stamp's caption and song. The photo, the place and the time can't change. */
export default function EditStamp() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const stamp = useStamp(id);
  if (!stamp.data) {
    return (
      <FormScreen>
        {stamp.isError ? <InlineError message={errorText(stamp.error, "Couldn't load this stamp.")} onRetry={() => void stamp.refetch()} retrying={stamp.isFetching} /> : <SkeletonRows count={3} />}
      </FormScreen>
    );
  }
  return <EditForm stamp={stamp.data.stamp} />;
}

function EditForm({ stamp }: { stamp: StampView }) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState(stamp.caption);
  const [song, setSong] = useState<Song | null>(stamp.song);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const change = stampChanges(stamp, caption, song);
  const dirty = Object.keys(change).length > 0;
  // A stamp with no photo must keep a caption or a song.
  const empty = stamp.photoUrl === null && caption.trim() === "" && song === null;
  useConfirmDiscard(dirty && !saved);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      const picked = songDraft.take();
      if (picked) setSong(picked.song);
    }, []),
  );

  const save = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.stamps.edit(stamp.id, change),
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      haptics.success();
      setSaved(true);
      patchStamp(queryClient, stamp.id, () => res.stamp);
      requestAnimationFrame(() => router.back());
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: "Edit stamp",
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerRight: () => <HeaderButton label="Save" bold onPress={() => save.mutate()} disabled={!dirty || empty || save.isPending} />,
        }}
      />
      <FieldGroup footer={`${caption.length}/${CAPTION_MAX}`} errors={[empty ? "Add a caption or a song, since this stamp has no photo." : null]}>
        <Field label="Caption" value={caption} onChangeText={setCaption} maxLength={CAPTION_MAX} placeholder="What's it like?" multiline />
      </FieldGroup>
      {song ? (
        <SongRow
          song={song}
          trailing={
            <Pressable onPress={() => setSong(null)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Remove song" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.remove}>
              <Icon name="xmark.circle.fill" color="textTertiary" size={size.icon} />
            </Pressable>
          }
        />
      ) : (
        <Button title="Add a song" icon="music.note" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/stamp/song")} />
      )}
      {save.error && <InlineError message={errorText(save.error, "Couldn't save. Try again.")} />}
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({ remove: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" } });


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/new.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router, Stack, useFocusEffect } from "expo-router";
import { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import { ActionSheetIOS, Pressable, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { CreateStampResponse, type NearbyPlacesResponse, type Song } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useNearbyPlaces, usePlaces } from "@/api/queries";
import { AccountGate } from "@/features/account/account-gate";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { devSimulator } from "@/features/location/dev-simulator";
import { locationStore, usePermissionRefresh, useLocationState, useUserCoords } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { SimulatedLocationChip } from "@/features/location/sim-chip";
import { CAMERA_NOTE, pickFromLibrary, takePhoto } from "@/features/photos/pick";
import { prepareImage } from "@/features/photos/prepare";
import { autoPlace, CAPTION_MAX, orderPlaces, placeDistanceLabel, stampFields, stampProblem } from "@/features/stamps/composer";
import { songDraft } from "@/features/stamps/song-draft";
import { SongRow } from "@/features/stamps/song-row";
import { formatDistance } from "@/lib/format";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { useConfirmDiscard } from "@/lib/use-confirm-discard";
import { colors, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

type Nearby = NearbyPlacesResponse["places"][number];

/**
 * The stamp composer. Pick the place you're at (the list comes from where your phone is; the server checks the
 * geofence again), add a photo, a caption, a song, any mix, then Stamp. If you're outside the geofence the server's
 * message, with how far away you are, is shown as it is.
 */
export default function NewStamp() {
  return (
    <AccountGate title="Stamp a place" message="Sign in to stamp the places you go.">
      <Composer />
    </AccountGate>
  );
}

function Composer() {
  usePermissionRefresh();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { permission } = useLocationState();
  const coords = useUserCoords();
  const nearby = useNearbyPlaces(coords);
  const [chosenId, setChosenId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [photoUri, setPhotoUri] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [preparing, setPreparing] = useState(false);
  const [photoNote, setPhotoNote] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [caption, setCaption] = useState("");
  const [song, setSong] = useState<Song | null>(null);
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0);
  const [done, setDone] = useState(false);

  const draft = { photoUri, caption, song };
  const dirty = !done && (photoUri !== null || caption.trim() !== "" || song !== null);
  useConfirmDiscard(dirty);

  const places = orderPlaces(nearby.data?.places ?? []);
  // The place you picked, or (when exactly one geofence has you inside) that one without asking.
  const place: Nearby | null = places.find((p) => p.id === chosenId) ?? autoPlace(places);

  // A song picked on the song screen comes back here when this screen regains focus.
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      const picked = songDraft.take();
      if (picked) setSong(picked.song);
    }, []),
  );

  const stamp = useMutation({
    mutationFn: async () => {
      if (!place) throw new Error("no place");
      // A fresh fix right now: the server checks these coordinates, not the ones the place list was built from.
      const here = (await locationStore.currentForPosting()) ?? coords;
      if (!here) throw new Error("no location");
      const form = new FormData();
      // React Native's FormData takes a file descriptor object for local files.
      if (photoUri) form.append("photo", { uri: photoUri, name: "photo.jpg", type: "image/jpeg" } as unknown as Blob);
      for (const [k, v] of stampFields(draft, here)) form.append(k, v);
      return api.upload(`/api/places/${encodeURIComponent(place.id)}/stamps`, CreateStampResponse, form, { onProgress: setProgress });
    },
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      haptics.success();
      setDone(true);
      toast.show("Stamped.");
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["feed"] });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user"] });
      requestAnimationFrame(() => router.replace({ pathname: "/stamp/[id]", params: { id: res.stamp.id } }));
    },
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const problem = stampProblem(draft, place?.id ?? null);

  const addPhoto = () => {
    setPhotoNote(null);
    ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
      { options: ["Take photo", "Choose photo", "Cancel"], cancelButtonIndex: 2, tintColor: colors.accent, userInterfaceStyle: "dark" },
      (i) => {
        if (i === 2) return;
        void (async () => {
          const picked = i === 0 ? await takePhoto() : await pickFromLibrary();
          if (picked === null || picked === "cancelled") return;
          if (typeof picked === "string") return setPhotoNote(CAMERA_NOTE[picked]);
          setPreparing(true);
          try {
            setPhotoUri((await prepareImage(picked.uri)).uri);
          } catch {
            setPhotoNote("Couldn't use that photo. Try another one.");
          } finally {
            setPreparing(false);
          }
        })();
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: "New stamp",
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerRight: () => <HeaderButton label="Stamp" bold onPress={() => stamp.mutate()} disabled={!!problem || stamp.isPending || preparing} />,
        }}
      />

      <View style={styles.section}>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" style={styles.heading}>
          WHERE ARE YOU?
        </Text>
        <SimulatedLocationChip />
        {permission !== "granted" && !coords ? (
          <View style={styles.notice}>
            <Icon name="location.fill" color="accent" size={size.iconLarge} />
            <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
              {permission === "denied" ? "Location is off. Turn it on in Settings to stamp the places you're at." : "Nostos checks that you're at a place before it gives you a stamp."}
            </Text>
            <Button title={permission === "denied" ? "Open settings" : "Use my location"} variant="secondary" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/location-permission")} />
          </View>
        ) : nearby.isPending || !coords ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={2} />
        ) : nearby.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(nearby.error, "Couldn't find places near you.")} onRetry={() => void nearby.refetch()} retrying={nearby.isFetching} />
        ) : places.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            No places near you yet. Nostos stamps places in New York.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup>
            {places.map((p) => (
              <ListRow
                key={p.id}
                icon={p.id === place?.id ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "circle"}
                iconColor={p.id === place?.id ? "accent" : "textTertiary"}
                title={p.name}
                subtitle={`${p.neighborhood} · ${placeDistanceLabel(p, formatDistance)}`}
                accessibilityLabel={`${p.name}, ${placeDistanceLabel(p, formatDistance)}${p.id === place?.id ? ", selected" : ""}`}
                onPress={() => setChosenId(p.id)}
              />
            ))}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
        {__DEV__ && devSimulator && <DevPlaces />}
      </View>

      <View style={styles.section}>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" style={styles.heading}>
          PHOTO, CAPTION OR SONG
        </Text>
        {photoUri ? (
          <View style={styles.photoWrap}>
            <Image source={{ uri: photoUri }} style={styles.photo} contentFit="cover" accessibilityLabel="Your photo" />
            <Pressable onPress={() => setPhotoUri(null)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Remove photo" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.removePhoto}>
              <Icon name="xmark" color="textPrimary" size={size.iconSmall} />
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        ) : (
          <Button title={preparing ? "Preparing photo…" : "Add a photo"} icon="camera.fill" variant="secondary" loading={preparing} onPress={addPhoto} />
        )}
        {photoNote && <InlineError message={photoNote} />}

        <FieldGroup footer={`${caption.length}/${CAPTION_MAX}`}>
          <Field label="Caption" value={caption} onChangeText={setCaption} maxLength={CAPTION_MAX} placeholder="What's it like?" multiline accessibilityHint={`Up to ${CAPTION_MAX} characters`} />
        </FieldGroup>

        {song ? (
          <SongRow
            song={song}
            trailing={
              <Pressable onPress={() => setSong(null)} accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel="Remove song" hitSlop={space.xs} style={styles.removeSong}>
                <Icon name="xmark.circle.fill" color="textTertiary" size={size.icon} />
              </Pressable>
            }
          />
        ) : (
          <Button title="Add a song" icon="music.note" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/stamp/song")} />
        )}
      </View>

      {problem && !stamp.isPending && (
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
          {problem}
        </Text>
      )}
      {stamp.isPending && (
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textSecondary" accessibilityLiveRegion="polite">
          {photoUri ? `Stamping… ${Math.round(progress * 100)}%` : "Stamping…"}
        </Text>
      )}
      {/* The server's own sentence, e.g. "You need to be within 100 m of this place to stamp it (you're 140 m away)". */}
      {stamp.error && <InlineError message={errorText(stamp.error, "Couldn't stamp that. Try again.")} onRetry={() => stamp.mutate()} retrying={stamp.isPending} />}
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

/** Development only: pretend to be at a place, to try the geofence without going there. */
function DevPlaces() {
  const places = usePlaces();
  if (!devSimulator) return null;
  const list = (places.data?.places ?? []).slice(0, 6);
  return (
    <ListGroup header="Development: pretend to be at">
      {list.map((p) => (
        <ListRow key={p.id} icon="location.slash" title={p.name} subtitle={p.neighborhood} onPress={() => devSimulator?.set({ lat: p.lat, lng: p.lng })} />
      ))}
    </ListGroup>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  section: { gap: space.sm },
  heading: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
  notice: { gap: space.sm, alignItems: "flex-start", padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card, backgroundColor: colors.surface },
  photoWrap: { borderRadius: radius.card, overflow: "hidden" },
  photo: { width: "100%", height: size.postPhotoSkeleton, backgroundColor: colors.elevated },
  removePhoto: { position: "absolute", top: space.xs, right: space.xs, width: size.hitTarget, height: size.hitTarget, borderRadius: radius.pill, backgroundColor: colors.scrim, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  removeSong: { minWidth: size.hitTarget, minHeight: size.hitTarget, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/app/(app)/stamp/song.tsx =====

import { Image } from "expo-image";
import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Pressable, ScrollView, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import type { Song, SongResult } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useSongSearch } from "@/api/queries";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { ListGroup, ListRow } from "@/components/ui/list-row";
import { SkeletonRows } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { songDraft } from "@/features/stamps/song-draft";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** Search songs (Apple's iTunes catalog, through Nostos) and pick one for the stamp. Metadata only: nothing plays. */
export default function SongPicker() {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const search = useSongSearch(query);

  // Search as you type, after a short pause.
  useEffect(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => setQuery(input.trim()), 350);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [input]);

  const choose = (r: SongResult) => {
    const song: Song = { title: r.title, artist: r.artist, artUrl: r.artUrl, sourceId: r.sourceId };
    songDraft.choose(song);
    router.back();
  };
  const songs = search.data?.songs ?? [];

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: "Add a song",
          headerLeft: () => <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />,
          headerSearchBarOptions: {
            placeholder: "Song or artist",
            autoCapitalize: "none",
            autoFocus: true,
            hideWhenScrolling: false,
            barTintColor: colors.surface,
            tintColor: colors.accent,
            textColor: colors.textPrimary,
            onChangeText: (e) => setInput(e.nativeEvent.text),
          },
        }}
      />
      <ScrollView contentInsetAdjustmentBehavior="automatic" keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled" keyboardDismissMode="on-drag" contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        {query.length < 2 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            Type a song or an artist.
          </Text>
        ) : search.isPending ? (
          <SkeletonRows count={4} />
        ) : search.isError ? (
          <InlineError message={errorText(search.error, "Couldn't search for songs right now.")} onRetry={() => void search.refetch()} retrying={search.isFetching} />
        ) : songs.length === 0 ? (
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            Nothing found for {"“"}
            {query}
            {"”"}.
          </Text>
        ) : (
          <ListGroup footer="Song info from Apple Music. Nostos doesn't play music.">
            {songs.map((s) => (
              <ListRow
                key={s.sourceId}
                leading={<Art url={s.artUrl} />}
                title={s.title}
                subtitle={[s.artist, s.album].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")}
                accessibilityLabel={`${s.title} by ${s.artist}`}
                accessibilityHint="Adds this song to your stamp"
                onPress={() => choose(s)}
              />
            ))}
          </ListGroup>
        )}
        <Pressable
          onPress={() => {
            songDraft.choose(null);
            router.back();
          }}
          accessibilityRole="button"
          accessibilityLabel="No song"
          style={styles.none}
        >
          <Text variant="callout" color="accent">
            No song
          </Text>
        </Pressable>
      </ScrollView>
    </View>
  );
}

function Art({ url }: { url: string | null }) {
  return url ? (
    <Image source={{ uri: url, cacheKey: url }} style={styles.art} contentFit="cover" transition={150} accessible={false} />
  ) : (
    <View style={styles.art}>
      <Icon name="music.note" color="textSecondary" size={size.icon} />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  content: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, paddingTop: space.md, paddingBottom: space.xxxl, gap: space.lg },
  art: { width: size.avatarLarge - space.md, height: size.avatarLarge - space.md, borderRadius: radius.chip, backgroundColor: colors.elevated, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center", overflow: "hidden" },
  none: { minHeight: size.hitTarget, justifyContent: "center" },
});
