===== src/app/_layout.tsx =====

import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router, Stack } from "expo-router";
import * as SplashScreen from "expo-splash-screen";
import { StatusBar } from "expo-status-bar";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet } from "react-native";
import { GestureHandlerRootView } from "react-native-gesture-handler";
import { SafeAreaProvider } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { createQueryClient, wireReactQueryToApp } from "@/api/query-client";
import { AuthProvider, useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { HeaderButton } from "@/components/ui/header-button";
import { stackOptions } from "@/components/ui/stack-options";
import { useOfflineAnnouncer } from "@/lib/network";
import { initErrorReporting } from "@/lib/report-error";
import { colors } from "@/theme";

void SplashScreen.preventAutoHideAsync().catch(() => undefined);
// Off unless the build sets EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_ENABLED=1 and a DSN (see lib/report-error.ts).
initErrorReporting();

export default function RootLayout() {
  const [queryClient] = useState(createQueryClient);
  useEffect(() => wireReactQueryToApp(), []);

  return (
    <GestureHandlerRootView style={styles.root}>
      <SafeAreaProvider>
        <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
          <AuthProvider>
            <StatusBar style="light" />
            <RootNavigator />
          </AuthProvider>
        </QueryClientProvider>
      </SafeAreaProvider>
    </GestureHandlerRootView>
  );
}

function RootNavigator() {
  useOfflineAnnouncer();
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const me = useMe();
  const signedIn = status === "signedIn";
  const meReady = signedIn && !!me.data;
  const needsTerms = meReady && me.data?.terms.needsAcceptance === true;
  const ready = status !== "loading" && !(signedIn && me.isLoading);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) SplashScreen.hide();
  }, [ready]);

  // Order matters: when a guard flips, the router lands on the first available screen.
  return (
    <Stack screenOptions={stackOptions}>
      <Stack.Protected guard={status === "signedOut" || status === "loading"}>
        <Stack.Screen name="welcome" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={signedIn && !meReady}>
        <Stack.Screen name="connect" options={{ headerShown: false, gestureEnabled: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={needsTerms}>
        <Stack.Screen name="terms-update" options={{ headerShown: false, gestureEnabled: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={status === "guest" || (meReady && !needsTerms)}>
        <Stack.Screen name="(app)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
      <Stack.Protected guard={!signedIn}>
        <Stack.Screen name="age-gate" options={{ title: "Age check", presentation: "modal", headerLeft: CloseButton }} />
        <Stack.Screen name="sign-in" options={{ title: "Sign in", presentation: "modal", headerLeft: CloseButton }} />
        <Stack.Screen name="sign-up" options={{ title: "Create account", presentation: "modal", headerLeft: CloseButton }} />
      </Stack.Protected>
    </Stack>
  );
}

function CloseButton() {
  return <HeaderButton label="Cancel" onPress={() => router.back()} />;
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({ root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background } });


===== src/app/age-gate.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { checkBirthdate, formatBirthdateInput } from "@/lib/age";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * The age gate for looking around without an account. The birthdate is checked here and thrown away: it isn't sent
 * anywhere or saved, and nothing is remembered about someone who isn't old enough.
 */
export default function AgeGate() {
  const { browseAsGuest } = useAuth();
  const [birthdate, setBirthdate] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [underage, setUnderage] = useState(false);

  const submit = () => {
    const check = checkBirthdate(birthdate);
    if (check.ok) {
      setMessage(null);
      browseAsGuest();
      return;
    }
    haptics.warning();
    setMessage(check.message);
    setUnderage(check.reason === "underage");
  };

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <View style={styles.intro}>
        <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
          Before you look around
        </Text>
        <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
          Nostos is for people 21 and older. Enter your birthdate. We check it on your phone and don&apos;t keep it.
        </Text>
      </View>
      <FieldGroup errors={[message]}>
        <Field
          label="Birthdate"
          placeholder="MM/DD/YYYY"
          value={birthdate}
          onChangeText={(v) => {
            setBirthdate(formatBirthdateInput(v));
            setMessage(null);
            setUnderage(false);
          }}
          invalid={!!message}
          keyboardType="number-pad"
          autoComplete="birthdate-full"
          maxLength={10}
          returnKeyType="done"
          onSubmitEditing={submit}
          testID="age-gate-birthdate"
        />
      </FieldGroup>
      {!underage && <Button title="Continue" onPress={submit} disabled={birthdate.length < 10} />}
      <Button title={underage ? "Close" : "Cancel"} variant="plain" onPress={() => router.back()} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({ intro: { gap: space.xs } });


===== src/app/connect.tsx =====

import { ActivityIndicator, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { errorText } from "@/api";
import { useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/brand/brand-mark";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * Signed in, but the account hasn't loaded yet (just signed in, or offline at launch). The app
 * waits here so the Terms check can't be skipped.
 */
export default function Connect() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const me = useMe();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const failed = me.isError || (me.isPending && me.fetchStatus === "paused");

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { paddingTop: insets.top + space.md, paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.md }]}>
      <BrandMark />
      <View style={styles.center}>
        {failed ? (
          <InlineError
            message={me.isError ? errorText(me.error, "Couldn't load your account. Try again.") : "You're offline. Connect to the internet to continue."}
            onRetry={() => void me.refetch()}
            retrying={me.isFetching}
          />
        ) : (
          <ActivityIndicator color={colors.textSecondary} accessibilityLabel="Loading your account" />
        )}
      </View>
      {failed && <Button title="Sign out" variant="plain" onPress={() => void signOut()} />}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  center: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "center" },
});


===== src/app/sign-in.tsx =====

import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { TextInput } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { isEmail } from "@/lib/validation";

export default function SignIn() {
  const { signIn } = useAuth();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [emailError, setEmailError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const passwordRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);

  const login = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.auth.login({ email: email.trim(), password }),
    onSuccess: (res) => signIn(res),
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const canSubmit = email.trim().length > 0 && password.length > 0;
  const submit = () => {
    if (!canSubmit || login.isPending) return;
    if (!isEmail(email)) {
      haptics.warning();
      setEmailError("Enter a valid email address.");
      return;
    }
    login.mutate();
  };

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <FieldGroup errors={[emailError]}>
        <Field
          label="Email"
          placeholder="name@example.com"
          value={email}
          invalid={!!emailError}
          onChangeText={(v) => {
            setEmail(v);
            setEmailError(null);
          }}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoComplete="email"
          textContentType="username"
          keyboardType="email-address"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => passwordRef.current?.focus()}
          autoFocus
        />
        <Field
          ref={passwordRef}
          label="Password"
          placeholder="Required"
          value={password}
          onChangeText={setPassword}
          secureTextEntry
          autoComplete="current-password"
          textContentType="password"
          returnKeyType="go"
          onSubmitEditing={submit}
        />
      </FieldGroup>
      {login.error && <InlineError message={errorText(login.error, "Couldn't sign in. Check your email and password and try again.")} />}
      <Button title="Sign in" onPress={submit} disabled={!canSubmit} loading={login.isPending} />
      <Button title="New here? Create an account" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.replace("/sign-up")} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}


===== src/app/sign-up.tsx =====

import { useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, TextInput, View } from "react-native";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { FormScreen } from "@/components/ui/form-screen";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { Field, FieldGroup } from "@/components/ui/text-field";
import { useAnnounce } from "@/lib/a11y";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { LEGAL, openWebPage } from "@/lib/links";
import { checkBirthdate, formatBirthdateInput } from "@/lib/age";
import { MIN_PASSWORD, validateSignup, type SignupErrors, type SignupFields } from "@/lib/validation";
import { space } from "@/theme";

export default function SignUp() {
  const { signIn } = useAuth();
  const [fields, setFields] = useState<SignupFields>({ email: "", password: "", handle: "", displayName: "", birthdate: "" });
  const [terms, setTerms] = useState(false);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<SignupErrors>({});
  const [consentError, setConsentError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const emailRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const passwordRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const handleRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const displayNameRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const birthdateRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  useAnnounce(consentError);

  const set = (k: keyof SignupFields) => (v: string) => {
    setFields((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: k === "handle" ? v.toLowerCase() : k === "birthdate" ? formatBirthdateInput(v) : v }));
    if (errors[k]) setErrors((e) => ({ ...e, [k]: undefined }));
  };

  const signup = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      api.auth.signup({
        email: fields.email.trim(),
        password: fields.password,
        handle: fields.handle.trim().toLowerCase(),
        displayName: fields.displayName.trim(),
        acceptTerms: true,
        // Checked on the phone first, and again by the server, which never stores it. An under-21 date never gets this far.
        birthdate: checkBirthdate(fields.birthdate).ok ? (checkBirthdate(fields.birthdate) as { apiDate: string }).apiDate : "",
      }),
    onSuccess: (res) => signIn(res),
    onError: () => haptics.warning(),
  });

  const submit = () => {
    if (signup.isPending) return;
    const e = validateSignup(fields);
    setErrors(e);
    const consent = !terms ? "Agree to the Terms and Privacy policy to continue." : null;
    setConsentError(consent);
    if (e.email) emailRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.password) passwordRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.handle) handleRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.displayName) displayNameRef.current?.focus();
    else if (e.birthdate) birthdateRef.current?.focus();
    if (Object.keys(e).length || consent) {
      haptics.warning();
      return;
    }
    signup.mutate();
  };

  return (
    <FormScreen>
      <FieldGroup errors={[errors.email, errors.password]} footer={`Passwords need at least ${MIN_PASSWORD} characters.`}>
        <Field
          ref={emailRef}
          label="Email"
          placeholder="name@example.com"
          value={fields.email}
          onChangeText={set("email")}
          invalid={!!errors.email}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoComplete="email"
          textContentType="username"
          keyboardType="email-address"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => passwordRef.current?.focus()}
        />
        <Field
          ref={passwordRef}
          label="Password"
          placeholder={`${MIN_PASSWORD}+ characters`}
          value={fields.password}
          onChangeText={set("password")}
          invalid={!!errors.password}
          secureTextEntry
          autoComplete="new-password"
          textContentType="newPassword"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => handleRef.current?.focus()}
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <FieldGroup errors={[errors.handle, errors.displayName]} footer="Your handle is shown on your stamps.">
        <Field
          ref={handleRef}
          label="Handle"
          placeholder="lowercase_name"
          value={fields.handle}
          onChangeText={set("handle")}
          invalid={!!errors.handle}
          autoCapitalize="none"
          autoCorrect={false}
          textContentType="none"
          autoComplete="off"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => displayNameRef.current?.focus()}
        />
        <Field
          ref={displayNameRef}
          label="Name"
          placeholder="Shown on your profile"
          value={fields.displayName}
          onChangeText={set("displayName")}
          invalid={!!errors.displayName}
          autoComplete="name"
          textContentType="name"
          returnKeyType="next"
          onSubmitEditing={() => birthdateRef.current?.focus()}
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <FieldGroup errors={[errors.birthdate]} footer="Nostos is for people 21 and older. We check your birthdate once and don't keep it.">
        <Field
          ref={birthdateRef}
          label="Birthdate"
          placeholder="MM/DD/YYYY"
          value={fields.birthdate}
          onChangeText={set("birthdate")}
          invalid={!!errors.birthdate}
          keyboardType="number-pad"
          autoComplete="birthdate-full"
          textContentType="none"
          maxLength={10}
          returnKeyType="done"
          testID="signup-birthdate"
        />
      </FieldGroup>

      <View style={styles.consents}>
        <Checkbox checked={terms} onChange={setTerms} invalid={!!consentError && !terms} accessibilityLabel="I agree to the Terms and Privacy policy">
          <Text variant="body">I agree to the Terms and Privacy policy</Text>
        </Checkbox>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
          There&apos;s no tolerance for abusive content or users. Anyone can report a stamp or an account.
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.links}>
          <Button title="Terms" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.terms)} accessibilityRole="link" />
          <Button title="Privacy policy" variant="plain" compact block={false} onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.privacy)} accessibilityRole="link" />
        </View>
        {consentError && (
          <Text variant="footnote" color="danger">
            {consentError}
          </Text>
        )}
      </View>

      {signup.error && <InlineError message={errorText(signup.error, "Couldn't create your account. Check the details and try again.")} />}
      <Button title="Create account" onPress={submit} loading={signup.isPending} />
      <Button title="Have an account? Sign in" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.replace("/sign-in")} />
    </FormScreen>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  consents: { gap: space.xs },
  links: { flexDirection: "row", marginLeft: -space.md },
});


===== src/app/terms-update.tsx =====

import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { api, errorText } from "@/api";
import { qk, useMe } from "@/api/queries";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { LEGAL, openWebPage } from "@/lib/links";
import { colors, screenPadding, space } from "@/theme";

/** Shown when the account accepted an older Terms version. */
export default function TermsUpdate() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { signOut } = useAuth();
  const me = useMe();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const version = me.data?.terms.currentVersion;

  const accept = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api.me.acceptTerms(version ?? ""),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.me }),
  });

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { paddingTop: insets.top + space.xxxl, paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.md }]}>
      <View style={styles.body}>
        <Text variant="title" accessibilityRole="header">
          Updated terms
        </Text>
        <Text color="textSecondary">We&apos;ve updated our Terms and Privacy policy. Review them to keep using Nostos.</Text>
        <View style={styles.links}>
          <Button title="Read the Terms" variant="plain" block={false} compact onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.terms)} />
          <Button title="Read the Privacy policy" variant="plain" block={false} compact onPress={() => openWebPage(LEGAL.privacy)} />
        </View>
      </View>
      <View style={styles.actions}>
        {accept.error && <InlineError message={errorText(accept.error, "Couldn't save that. Try again.")} />}
        <Button title="Agree and continue" onPress={() => accept.mutate()} loading={accept.isPending} disabled={!version} />
        <Button title="Sign out" variant="plain" onPress={() => void signOut()} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, paddingHorizontal: screenPadding, justifyContent: "space-between" },
  body: { gap: space.sm },
  links: { marginTop: space.xs, marginLeft: -space.md },
  actions: { gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/app/welcome.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import MapView from "react-native-maps";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { farewellNotice } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { BrandMark } from "@/components/brand/brand-mark";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { useAnnounce } from "@/lib/a11y";
import { HOME_REGION } from "@/lib/geo";
import { colors, radius, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

export default function Welcome() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [panelHeight, setPanelHeight] = useState(0);
  // Shown once after deleting an account.
  const [farewell] = useState(() => farewellNotice.take());
  useAnnounce(farewell);

  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      <MapView
        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
        initialRegion={HOME_REGION}
        userInterfaceStyle="dark"
        showsPointsOfInterests={false}
        showsCompass={false}
        pitchEnabled={false}
        scrollEnabled={false}
        zoomEnabled={false}
        rotateEnabled={false}
        // Keep Apple's logo and Legal link visible above the panel.
        legalLabelInsets={{ top: 0, left: space.md, right: 0, bottom: panelHeight + space.xs }}
        accessibilityElementsHidden
        importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants"
      />
      <View style={[styles.top, { paddingTop: insets.top + space.xs }]}>
        <BrandMark />
      </View>
      <View style={[styles.panel, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + space.sm }]} onLayout={(e) => setPanelHeight(e.nativeEvent.layout.height)}>
        {farewell && (
          <View style={styles.farewell}>
            <Icon name="checkmark.circle.fill" color="success" size={size.iconSmall} />
            <Text variant="subhead" weight="semibold">
              {farewell}
            </Text>
          </View>
        )}
        <Text variant="largeTitle" accessibilityRole="header">
          Go somewhere. Get a stamp.
        </Text>
        <Text variant="body" color="textSecondary">
          Your passport for New York: a photo, a few words or a song from every place you go. Only people you approve see it.
        </Text>
        <View style={styles.actions}>
          <Button title="Create account" onPress={() => router.push("/sign-up")} />
          <Button title="Sign in" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push("/sign-in")} />
          <Button title="Look around first" variant="plain" onPress={() => router.push("/age-gate")} accessibilityHint="Asks your birthdate, then lets you browse without an account" />
        </View>
        <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary">
          For adults 21 and over.
        </Text>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background },
  top: { paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  panel: {
    marginTop: "auto",
    gap: space.sm,
    paddingTop: space.xl,
    paddingHorizontal: screenPadding,
    backgroundColor: colors.background,
    borderTopLeftRadius: radius.sheet,
    borderTopRightRadius: radius.sheet,
  },
  actions: { gap: space.xs, marginTop: space.sm },
  farewell: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", gap: space.xs },
});


===== src/features/account/__tests__/account-gate.test.tsx =====

/** "Sign in to post" must never show while the account is still loading or failed to load. */
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";

import { accountState } from "@/auth/account-state";
import { AccountGate } from "../account-gate";

let mockState: ReturnType<typeof import("@/auth/account-state").useAccountState>;
const retry = jest.fn();
jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/account-state", () => ({ ...jest.requireActual("@/auth/account-state"), useAccountState: () => mockState }));

describe("accountState", () => {
  const me = (isSuccess: boolean, isError: boolean) => ({ isSuccess, isError });
  it("is checking while auth or /api/me is loading, never 'guest'", () => {
    expect(accountState("loading", me(false, false))).toBe("checking");
    expect(accountState("signedIn", me(false, false))).toBe("checking");
  });
  it("is an error when signed in but /api/me failed", () => {
    expect(accountState("signedIn", me(false, true))).toBe("error");
  });
  it("is a guest only when there is no account, and ready once /api/me loaded", () => {
    expect(accountState("signedOut", me(false, false))).toBe("guest");
    expect(accountState("guest", me(false, false))).toBe("guest");
    expect(accountState("signedIn", me(true, false))).toBe("ready");
  });
});

describe("AccountGate", () => {
  const render_ = async () => render(<AccountGate title="Stamp a place" message="Sign in to stamp the places you go."><></></AccountGate>);

  it("checking: says so, and offers no sign-in", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "checking", retry, retrying: false };
    await render_();
    expect(screen.getByText("Checking your account")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Sign in")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/Sign in to/)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("error: offers Try again, not Sign in", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "error", retry, retrying: false };
    await render_();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Couldn't check your account/)).toBeTruthy();
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Try again"));
    expect(retry).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Sign in")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("guest: the sign-in prompt", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "guest", retry, retrying: false };
    await render_();
    expect(screen.getByText("Stamp a place")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByText("Sign in")).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it("ready: shows the screen", async () => {
    mockState = { state: "ready", retry, retrying: false };
    await render(<AccountGate title="t" message="m"><></></AccountGate>);
    expect(screen.queryByText("Checking your account")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Sign in")).toBeNull();
  });
});


===== src/features/account/__tests__/age-gate-screens.test.tsx =====

/** The 21+ gate: the sign-up form and the guest gate turn away under-21s and keep nothing. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import * as SecureStore from "expo-secure-store";

import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";
import AgeGate from "@/app/age-gate";
import SignUp from "@/app/sign-up";

const mockBrowse = jest.fn();
const mockSignup = jest.fn();
const mockSignIn = jest.fn();

jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn(), back: jest.fn(), replace: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ browseAsGuest: mockBrowse, signIn: mockSignIn }) }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => {
  const client = jest.requireActual("@/api/client");
  return { api: { auth: { signup: (b: unknown) => mockSignup(b) } }, ApiError: client.ApiError, errorText: client.errorText };
});

const wrap = async (ui: React.ReactElement) => render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}>{ui}</QueryClientProvider>);
const yearsAgo = (years: number, dayOffset = 0) => {
  const d = new Date();
  d.setFullYear(d.getFullYear() - years);
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + dayOffset);
  const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0");
  const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, "0");
  return `${mm}${dd}${d.getFullYear()}`;
};

beforeEach(() => {
  mockBrowse.mockClear();
  mockSignup.mockReset();
  mockSignIn.mockClear();
  jest.mocked(SecureStore.setItemAsync).mockClear();
});

describe("guest age gate", () => {
  it("lets in someone who is 21 today, and formats the date as they type", async () => {
    await wrap(<AgeGate />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate"), yearsAgo(21));
    expect(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate").props.value).toMatch(/^\d{2}\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Continue"));
    expect(mockBrowse).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("turns away someone a day short of 21: one sentence, no way forward, nothing saved", async () => {
    await wrap(<AgeGate />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate"), yearsAgo(21, 1));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Continue"));
    expect(mockBrowse).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getByText("Nostos is for people 21 and older.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByText("Continue")).toBeNull();
    expect(SecureStore.setItemAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("asks again for an impossible date instead of guessing", async () => {
    await wrap(<AgeGate />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("age-gate-birthdate"), "02301990");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Continue"));
    expect(mockBrowse).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getByText("Enter your birthdate as MM/DD/YYYY.")).toBeTruthy();
  });
});

describe("sign-up age gate", () => {
  async function fill(birthdate: string) {
    await wrap(<SignUp />);
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Email"), "sam@example.com");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Password"), "long enough pw");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Handle"), "sam_s");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByLabelText("Name"), "Sam");
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("signup-birthdate"), birthdate);
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("I agree to the Terms and Privacy policy"));
  }

  it("has a birthdate field in place of the old 21+ checkbox", async () => {
    await wrap(<SignUp />);
    expect(screen.getByTestId("signup-birthdate")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("I'm 21 or older")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("sends the birthdate to the server once, as YYYY-MM-DD, when the person is 21 or older", async () => {
    mockSignup.mockResolvedValue({ token: "t", expiresAt: "2027-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", user: { id: "u", handle: "sam_s", displayName: "Sam", role: "USER" } });
    await fill("05051990");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Create account"));
    expect(mockSignup).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(mockSignup.mock.calls[0][0]).toMatchObject({ email: "sam@example.com", birthdate: "1990-05-05", acceptTerms: true });
    expect(mockSignup.mock.calls[0][0]).not.toHaveProperty("confirm21");
  });

  it("never sends an under-21 birthdate: it stops on the phone and says why", async () => {
    await fill(yearsAgo(20));
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByText("Create account"));
    expect(mockSignup).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(screen.getAllByText("Nostos is for people 21 and older.").length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });
});


===== src/features/account/account-gate.tsx =====

import { router } from "expo-router";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { useAccountState } from "@/auth/account-state";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { InlineError } from "@/components/ui/inline-error";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { colors, screenPadding, size, space } from "@/theme";

/**
 * Shows `children` once the account is ready. While the account is still loading it says so ("Checking your account"),
 * when /api/me failed it offers Try again, and only a real guest gets "Sign in". Screens that need an account use this
 * so a slow or failed load never reads as "you're signed out".
 */
export function AccountGate({ title, message, children }: { title: string; message: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { state, retry, retrying } = useAccountState();
  if (state === "ready") return <>{children}</>;
  return (
    <View style={styles.root}>
      {state === "checking" && (
        <View style={styles.block} accessibilityRole="progressbar" accessibilityLabel="Checking your account">
          <Skeleton width={size.avatarLarge} height={size.avatarLarge} round />
          <Text variant="headline">Checking your account</Text>
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary">
            One moment.
          </Text>
        </View>
      )}
      {state === "error" && (
        <View style={styles.block}>
          <InlineError message="Couldn't check your account. Your connection may be down." onRetry={retry} retrying={retrying} />
        </View>
      )}
      {state === "guest" && (
        <View style={styles.block}>
          <Icon name="person.crop.circle" color="textSecondary" size={size.iconLarge} />
          <Text variant="title2" accessibilityRole="header">
            {title}
          </Text>
          <Text variant="callout" color="textSecondary" style={styles.center}>
            {message}
          </Text>
          <Button title="Sign in" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/sign-in")} />
          <Button title="Create account" variant="plain" block={false} onPress={() => router.push("/sign-up")} />
        </View>
      )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: colors.background, justifyContent: "center", paddingHorizontal: screenPadding },
  block: { alignItems: "center", gap: space.sm },
  center: { textAlign: "center" },
});


===== src/features/location/__tests__/dev-simulator.test.tsx =====

/* eslint-disable @typescript-eslint/no-require-imports -- jest.isolateModules needs require() */
/**
 * The location simulator must be impossible to reach in production builds, where `__DEV__` is false.
 */
jest.mock("expo-location", () => ({
  PermissionStatus: { GRANTED: "granted", UNDETERMINED: "undetermined", DENIED: "denied" },
  Accuracy: { High: 4 },
  getForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(),
  requestForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(),
  watchPositionAsync: jest.fn(),
  getCurrentPositionAsync: jest.fn(),
}));

type G = { __DEV__: boolean };

function loadWithDev(dev: boolean) {
  const g = globalThis as unknown as G;
  const previous = g.__DEV__;
  g.__DEV__ = dev;
  let mods!: {
    sim: typeof import("../dev-simulator");
    store: typeof import("../location-store");
    chip: typeof import("../sim-chip");
    rtl: typeof import("@testing-library/react-native/pure");
    React: typeof import("react");
  };
  jest.isolateModules(() => {
    mods = {
      sim: require("../dev-simulator"),
      store: require("../location-store"),
      chip: require("../sim-chip"),
      // Same module registry as the component, so there is one copy of React.
      rtl: require("@testing-library/react-native/pure"),
      React: require("react"),
    };
  });
  return {
    ...mods,
    restore: () => {
      g.__DEV__ = previous;
    },
  };
}

describe("dev location simulator", () => {
  it("works in development", () => {
    const { sim, store, restore } = loadWithDev(true);
    try {
      expect(sim.devSimulator).not.toBeNull();
      expect(sim.simulateAtPress).toBeDefined();
      sim.simulateAtPress!({ latitude: 40.72, longitude: -73.98 });
      expect(store.locationStore.current()).toEqual({ lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 });
      sim.devSimulator!.clear();
      expect(store.locationStore.get().simulated).toBeNull();
    } finally {
      restore();
    }
  });

  it("does not exist in production builds", async () => {
    const { sim, store, chip, rtl, React, restore } = loadWithDev(false);
    try {
      expect(sim.devSimulator).toBeNull();
      // No long-press handler is attached to the map at all.
      expect(sim.simulateAtPress).toBeUndefined();
      // Even the store's internal setter refuses.
      store.locationStore._setSimulated({ lat: 1, lng: 2 });
      expect(store.locationStore.get().simulated).toBeNull();
      expect(store.locationStore.current()).toBeNull();
      // And the chip never renders.
      const screen = await rtl.render(React.createElement(chip.SimulatedLocationChip));
      expect(screen.queryByTestId("sim-chip")).toBeNull();
      expect(screen.queryByText("Simulated location")).toBeNull();
    } finally {
      restore();
    }
  });
});


===== src/features/location/__tests__/location-store.test.ts =====

import { locationStore, SORT_ANCHOR_M } from "../location-store";

const mockListeners: ((p: { coords: { latitude: number; longitude: number; accuracy: number }; timestamp: number }) => void)[] = [];

jest.mock("expo-location", () => ({
  PermissionStatus: { GRANTED: "granted", UNDETERMINED: "undetermined", DENIED: "denied" },
  Accuracy: { High: 4 },
  getForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(async () => ({ status: "granted", canAskAgain: true })),
  requestForegroundPermissionsAsync: jest.fn(),
  watchPositionAsync: jest.fn(async (_opts: unknown, cb: (typeof mockListeners)[number]) => {
    mockListeners.push(cb);
    return { remove: jest.fn() };
  }),
  getCurrentPositionAsync: jest.fn(),
}));

const move = (lat: number, lng: number) => mockListeners.forEach((cb) => cb({ coords: { latitude: lat, longitude: lng, accuracy: 5 }, timestamp: Date.now() }));

describe("sort anchor", () => {
  it("only moves after the user travels more than the threshold", async () => {
    await locationStore.refreshPermission();
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    move(40.72, -73.98);
    const first = locationStore.get().anchor;
    expect(first).toEqual({ lat: 40.72, lng: -73.98 });

    // ~11 m north: same anchor object, so memoized sorts don't rerun.
    move(40.7201, -73.98);
    expect(locationStore.get().anchor).toBe(first);
    expect(locationStore.current()).toEqual({ lat: 40.7201, lng: -73.98 });

    // ~111 m north: anchor follows.
    move(40.721, -73.98);
    expect(locationStore.get().anchor).toEqual({ lat: 40.721, lng: -73.98 });
    expect(SORT_ANCHOR_M).toBe(50);
  });
});


===== src/features/location/dev-simulator.ts =====

/**
 * Development-only location simulator: long-press the map to pretend you're there.
 *
 * `devSimulator` is `null` whenever `__DEV__` is false. Release builds are minified with
 * `__DEV__ === false`, so the branch below is removed and nothing can set a fake location.
 * The server still enforces the geofence against whatever coordinates arrive.
 */
import type { Coords } from "@/lib/geo";
import { locationStore } from "./location-store";

export type DevSimulator = {
  set: (coords: Coords) => void;
  clear: () => void;
};

export const devSimulator: DevSimulator | null = __DEV__
  ? {
      set: (coords) => locationStore._setSimulated(coords),
      clear: () => locationStore._setSimulated(null),
    }
  : null;

/** Map long-press handler, or undefined outside development (the map then has no long-press at all). */
export const simulateAtPress: ((coordinate: { latitude: number; longitude: number }) => void) | undefined = devSimulator
  ? (c) => devSimulator!.set({ lat: c.latitude, lng: c.longitude })
  : undefined;


===== src/features/location/location-store.ts =====

/**
 * The user's location, for display (Nearby sort, map dot) and for the coordinates sent with
 * reports, photos and presence pings. The server decides the geofence from those coordinates.
 *
 * Permission is when-in-use only, and only requested after the pre-permission screen.
 */
import * as Location from "expo-location";
import { useEffect, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { AppState } from "react-native";

import { distanceMeters, type Coords } from "@/lib/geo";

export type PermissionState = "unknown" | "undetermined" | "granted" | "denied";

type State = {
  permission: PermissionState;
  /** Real GPS fix. */
  device: (Coords & { accuracy: number | null; at: number }) | null;
  /** Development-only override (see dev-simulator.ts). Always null in release builds. */
  simulated: Coords | null;
  /** Position for distance sorting; moves only in steps of SORT_ANCHOR_M. */
  anchor: Coords | null;
};

export const SORT_ANCHOR_M = 50;

let state: State = { permission: "unknown", device: null, simulated: null, anchor: null };
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
let watcher: Location.LocationSubscription | null = null;

function set(patch: Partial<Omit<State, "anchor">>) {
  const next = { ...state, ...patch };
  const live = next.simulated ?? (next.device ? { lat: next.device.lat, lng: next.device.lng } : null);
  let anchor = state.anchor;
  if (!live) anchor = null;
  else if (!anchor || patch.simulated !== undefined || distanceMeters(anchor, live) > SORT_ANCHOR_M) anchor = live;
  state = { ...next, anchor };
  for (const l of listeners) l();
}

function toPermission(status: Location.PermissionStatus, canAskAgain: boolean): PermissionState {
  if (status === Location.PermissionStatus.GRANTED) return "granted";
  if (status === Location.PermissionStatus.UNDETERMINED || canAskAgain) return "undetermined";
  return "denied";
}

async function startWatching() {
  if (watcher) return;
  try {
    watcher = await Location.watchPositionAsync(
      { accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High, distanceInterval: 10, timeInterval: 5000 },
      (pos) => set({ device: { lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude, accuracy: pos.coords.accuracy, at: pos.timestamp } }),
    );
  } catch {
    watcher = null;
  }
}

export const locationStore = {
  get: () => state,
  subscribe(l: () => void) {
    listeners.add(l);
    return () => listeners.delete(l);
  },

  /** Read the current permission without prompting; start watching if already granted. */
  async refreshPermission(): Promise<PermissionState> {
    const res = await Location.getForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    const permission = toPermission(res.status, res.canAskAgain);
    set({ permission });
    if (permission === "granted") void startWatching();
    return permission;
  },

  /** Show the system prompt. Call only from the pre-permission screen. */
  async request(): Promise<PermissionState> {
    const res = await Location.requestForegroundPermissionsAsync();
    const permission = toPermission(res.status, res.canAskAgain);
    set({ permission });
    if (permission === "granted") void startWatching();
    return permission;
  },

  /** Where to send as the user's position: the simulated point in development, else the device fix. */
  current(): Coords | null {
    if (state.simulated) return state.simulated;
    return state.device ? { lat: state.device.lat, lng: state.device.lng } : null;
  },

  /** A fresh fix for posting (falls back to the last watched one). */
  async currentForPosting(): Promise<Coords | null> {
    if (state.simulated) return state.simulated;
    if (state.permission !== "granted") return null;
    try {
      const pos = await Location.getCurrentPositionAsync({ accuracy: Location.Accuracy.High });
      const device = { lat: pos.coords.latitude, lng: pos.coords.longitude, accuracy: pos.coords.accuracy, at: pos.timestamp };
      set({ device });
      return { lat: device.lat, lng: device.lng };
    } catch {
      return locationStore.current();
    }
  },

  /** @internal Used by dev-simulator.ts only. */
  _setSimulated(simulated: Coords | null) {
    if (!__DEV__) return;
    set({ simulated });
  },
};

export function useLocationState(): State {
  return useSyncExternalStore(locationStore.subscribe, locationStore.get, locationStore.get);
}

/** The effective position for display: simulated (dev) or device. */
export function useUserCoords(): Coords | null {
  const s = useLocationState();
  if (s.simulated) return s.simulated;
  return s.device ? { lat: s.device.lat, lng: s.device.lng } : null;
}

/** Re-read the permission when the app returns to the foreground (e.g. back from Settings). */
export function usePermissionRefresh(): void {
  useEffect(() => {
    void locationStore.refreshPermission();
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener("change", (s) => {
      if (s === "active") void locationStore.refreshPermission();
    });
    return () => sub.remove();
  }, []);
}

/**
 * The user's position for sorting by distance. It only changes after they move more than
 * `SORT_ANCHOR_M`, so lists don't reshuffle under a finger on every GPS tick. Stable identity.
 */
export function useSortCoords(): Coords | null {
  return useSyncExternalStore(locationStore.subscribe, () => state.anchor, () => state.anchor);
}


===== src/features/location/sim-chip.tsx =====

import { Pressable, StyleSheet } from "react-native";

import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { Text } from "@/components/ui/text";
import { devSimulator } from "@/features/location/dev-simulator";
import { useLocationState } from "@/features/location/location-store";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, radius, size, space } from "@/theme";

/** "Simulated location" chip that clears the fake location. Renders nothing outside development. */
export function SimulatedLocationChip() {
  const { simulated } = useLocationState();
  if (!devSimulator || !simulated) return null;
  return (
    <Pressable
      onPress={devSimulator.clear}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel="Simulated location. Clear it."
      style={({ pressed }) => [styles.chip, pressed && styles.pressed]}
      hitSlop={space.xs}
      testID="sim-chip"
    >
      <Icon name="location.slash" color="warning" size={size.iconTiny} />
      <Text variant="footnote" weight="semibold">
        Simulated location
      </Text>
      <Icon name="xmark" color="textSecondary" size={size.iconTiny} />
    </Pressable>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  chip: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    alignSelf: "flex-start",
    gap: space.xs,
    minHeight: size.hitTarget - space.xs,
    paddingHorizontal: space.sm,
    borderRadius: radius.pill,
    backgroundColor: colors.elevated,
    borderWidth: hairlineWidth,
    borderColor: colors.hairline,
  },
  pressed: { backgroundColor: colors.surface },
});

===== src/features/moderation/__tests__/guideline-1-2.test.tsx =====

/** Apple Guideline 1.2: every stamp and every profile can be reported, and its author blocked, in the app. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { ActionSheetIOS } from "react-native";

import type { StampView } from "@/api/contracts";
import { StampCard } from "@/features/stamps/stamp-card";
import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";

jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn(), back: jest.fn() } }));
jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => ({ useAuth: () => ({ status: "signedIn" }) }));
jest.mock("@/api", () => ({ api: {}, mediaUrl: (u: string) => u, errorText: (_e: unknown, f: string) => f }));

const stamp = (over: Partial<StampView> = {}): StampView => ({
  id: "s1", photoUrl: null, caption: "hello", song: null, createdAt: "2026-09-24T02:00:00.000Z", editedAt: null,
  place: { id: "p1", slug: "velvet", name: "Velvet", neighborhood: "East Village" },
  author: { handle: "sam", displayName: "Sam", avatarUrl: null, verified: false }, mine: false, likes: { count: 0, viewerLiked: false }, hidden: false, removed: false, ...over,
});

    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("More options"));

    expect(menu.options).toEqual(["Report stamp", "Block @sam", "Cancel"]);
    expect(menu.destructiveButtonIndex).toEqual([0, 1]);
    // Choosing Report opens the reasons.

    const reasons = sheet.mock.calls[1]![0].options as string[];
    expect(reasons).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["Under 21 or looks underage", "Stalking or unwanted contact", "Not at this place"]));
    sheet.mockRestore();
  });

  it("your own stamp has no Report or Block (you can edit or delete it from its detail screen)", async () => {
    await render(<QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}><StampCard stamp={stamp({ mine: true })} /></QueryClientProvider>);
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText("More options")).toBeNull();
  });
});


===== src/features/moderation/__tests__/reasons.test.ts =====

  it("include underage, stalking and not at this place, each with its category", () => {

===== src/features/moderation/content-actions.ts =====

/**
 * Report and Block for user content (Guideline 1.2): every stamp and profile can be reported, and every author
 * blocked. Native action sheets and alerts.
 */
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { router } from "expo-router";
import { ActionSheetIOS, Alert } from "react-native";

import { api, ApiError, errorText } from "@/api";
import type { FlagCategory, TargetType } from "@/api/contracts";
import { useAuth } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import { toast } from "@/components/ui/toast";
import { haptics } from "@/lib/haptics";
import { colors } from "@/theme";
import { flaggedStore } from "./flagged-store";

/** What a person can say is wrong. The server stores the label as the reason and the category for moderators. */
export const REPORT_REASONS: readonly { label: string; category: FlagCategory }[] = [
  { label: "Spam or scam", category: "SPAM" },
  { label: "Harassment or hate", category: "HARASSMENT" },
  { label: "Sexual or violent content", category: "SEXUAL_OR_VIOLENT" },
  { label: "Not at this place", category: "NOT_AT_PLACE" },
  { label: "Stalking or unwanted contact", category: "STALKING" },
  { label: "Under 21 or looks underage", category: "UNDERAGE" },
  { label: "Someone's private information", category: "PRIVATE_INFO" },
];

export type ChosenReason = { reason: string; category: FlagCategory };

export function useFlag() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { targetType: TargetType; targetId: string; reason: string; category: FlagCategory }) => api.flags.create(v),
    onSuccess: (res, v) => {
      haptics.success();
      if (v.targetType === "STAMP") flaggedStore.add(v.targetId);
      toast.show(res.autoHidden ? "Thanks. It's hidden while we review it." : "Thanks. We'll take a look.");
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["feed"] });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["user"] });
    },
    onError: (e, v) => {
      haptics.warning();
      // Already flagged (409): hide it for this person anyway. Limits (429) come with the server's sentence.
      if (e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 409 && v.targetType === "STAMP") flaggedStore.add(v.targetId);
      toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't send that report. Try again."), "neutral");
    },
  });
}

/** Block or unblock a person by handle. */
export function useBlock() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (v: { handle: string; block: boolean }) => api.users.block(v.handle, v.block),
    onSuccess: (_r, v) => {
      toast.show(v.block ? `Blocked @${v.handle}.` : `Unblocked @${v.handle}.`);
      // Blocking hides each other's stamps everywhere.
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    },
    onError: (e) => toast.show(errorText(e, "Couldn't do that. Try again."), "neutral"),
  });
}

/** Ask for a reason, then flag. */
export function chooseReportReason(
  title: string,
  onPick: (chosen: ChosenReason) => void,
  reasons: readonly { label: string; category: FlagCategory }[] = REPORT_REASONS,
  message = "Reports are reviewed by moderators. The person isn't told who reported them.",
) {
  ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
    {
      title,
      message,
      options: [...reasons.map((r) => r.label), "Cancel"],
      cancelButtonIndex: reasons.length,
      tintColor: colors.accent,
      userInterfaceStyle: "dark",
    },
    (i) => {
      const r = reasons[i];
      if (r) onPick({ reason: r.label, category: r.category });
    },
  );
}

export function confirmBlock(handle: string, onConfirm: () => void) {
  Alert.alert(`Block @${handle}?`, "You won't see each other's stamps or profile, and follows are removed. They aren't notified.", [
    { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
    { text: "Block", style: "destructive", onPress: onConfirm },
  ]);
}

export type StampMenuItem = {
  id: string;
  authorHandle: string;
  mine: boolean;
  /** Extra actions shown first (Edit, Delete from profile). */
  extra?: ContentAction[];
};

export type ContentAction = { label: string; destructive?: boolean; onPress: () => void };

/** The actions for a stamp: extras (Edit, Delete) first, then Report and Block for others' stamps. */
export function useStampActions(): (item: StampMenuItem) => ContentAction[] {
  const { status } = useAuth();
  const flag = useFlag();
  const block = useBlock();

  return (item) => {
    const actions: ContentAction[] = [...(item.extra ?? [])];
    if (!item.mine) {
      actions.push({
        label: "Report stamp",
        destructive: true,
        onPress: () => {
          if (status !== "signedIn") return router.push("/sign-in");
          chooseReportReason("Report this stamp", (chosen) => flag.mutate({ targetType: "STAMP", targetId: item.id, ...chosen }));
        },
      });
      actions.push({
        label: `Block @${item.authorHandle}`,
        destructive: true,
        onPress: () => {
          if (status !== "signedIn") return router.push("/sign-in");
          confirmBlock(item.authorHandle, () => block.mutate({ handle: item.authorHandle, block: true }));
        },
      });
    }
    return actions;
  };
}

/** Native action sheet for a list of actions. */
export function showActions(actions: ContentAction[]): void {
  if (actions.length === 0) return;
  haptics.select();
  ActionSheetIOS.showActionSheetWithOptions(
    {
      options: [...actions.map((a) => a.label), "Cancel"],
      cancelButtonIndex: actions.length,
      destructiveButtonIndex: actions.map((a, i) => (a.destructive ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0),
      tintColor: colors.accent,
      userInterfaceStyle: "dark",
    },
    (i) => actions[i]?.onPress(),
  );
}

/** The same actions in VoiceOver's actions rotor, so nothing depends on long-press. */
export function a11yActions(actions: ContentAction[]) {
  return {
    accessibilityActions: actions.map((a) => ({ name: a.label, label: a.label })),
    onAccessibilityAction: (e: { nativeEvent: { actionName: string } }) => actions.find((a) => a.label === e.nativeEvent.actionName)?.onPress(),
  };
}

export function isApiError(e: unknown, status: number): e is ApiError {
  return e instanceof ApiError && e.status === status;
}


===== src/features/moderation/flagged-store.ts =====

/**
 * Content this person has reported during the session. Hidden from them right away (Guideline 1.2:
 * reported content shouldn't keep confronting the reporter); moderators decide what happens for
 * everyone else.
 */
import { useSyncExternalStore } from "react";

let ids = new Set<string>();
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

export const flaggedStore = {
  add(id: string) {
    ids = new Set(ids).add(id);
    listeners.forEach((l) => l());
  },
  has: (id: string) => ids.has(id),
  subscribe(l: () => void) {
    listeners.add(l);
    return () => listeners.delete(l);
  },
  get: () => ids,
};

export function useFlaggedIds(): Set<string> {
  return useSyncExternalStore(flaggedStore.subscribe, flaggedStore.get, flaggedStore.get);
}


===== src/features/moderation/format.ts =====

/** Moderation copy: the API's raw actions, in words. */
import type { z } from "zod";

import type { ModerationAction as ModerationActionSchema } from "@/api/contracts";

type ModerationAction = z.infer<typeof ModerationActionSchema>;

const ACTION_WORD: Record<ModerationAction, string> = {
  FLAGGED: "Flagged",
  AUTO_HIDE: "Auto-hidden",
  HIDE: "Hidden",
  DISMISS: "Dismissed",
  RESTORE: "Restored",
  ACCOUNT_DELETED: "Account deleted",
  SUSPEND: "Suspended",
  UNSUSPEND: "Unsuspended",
};

export function actionWord(a: ModerationAction): string {
  return ACTION_WORD[a];
}

/** Reputation (0.2 to 1.5, new users 0.5) as a percentage of the default. */
export function reputationText(rep: number): string {
  return `trust ${Math.round((rep / 0.5) * 100)}%`;
}


===== src/features/people/__tests__/delete-account.test.tsx =====

/** Guideline 5.1.1(v): account deletion works end to end from the in-app screen. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { act, fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react-native";
import { Alert } from "react-native";

import { ApiError } from "@/api/client";
import { farewellNotice } from "@/auth/auth-context";
import DeleteAccount from "@/app/(app)/settings/delete-account";
import { testQueryClient } from "@/test/query-client";

const mockForget = jest.fn(async () => undefined);
const mockDelete = jest.fn();

jest.mock("@/auth/auth-context", () => {
  const actual = jest.requireActual("@/auth/auth-context");
  return { useAuth: () => ({ forget: mockForget }), farewellNotice: actual.farewellNotice };
});
jest.mock("@/api", () => {
  const client = jest.requireActual("@/api/client");
  return { api: { me: { deleteAccount: (pw: string) => mockDelete(pw) } }, ApiError: client.ApiError, errorText: client.errorText };
});

async function renderScreen() {
  return render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={testQueryClient()}>
      <DeleteAccount />
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
}

/** Press the destructive button in the confirmation alert. */
const alert = jest.spyOn(Alert, "alert").mockImplementation((_t, _m, buttons) => {
  buttons?.find((b) => b.style === "destructive")?.onPress?.();
});

const flush = () => act(() => new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, 0)));

describe("Delete account", () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    mockForget.mockClear();
    mockDelete.mockReset();
    alert.mockClear();
  });

  it("explains what happens and needs a password", async () => {
    await renderScreen();
    expect(screen.getByText("Your stamps are removed from every passport, and so are your follows and likes.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account")).toBeDisabled();
  });

  it("confirms, calls the API with the password, then signs out locally", async () => {
    mockDelete.mockResolvedValue({ deleted: true });
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "correct horse battery");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    await flush();
    expect(alert).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Delete your account?", "This can't be undone.", expect.any(Array));
    expect(mockDelete).toHaveBeenCalledWith("correct horse battery");
    await flush();
    expect(mockForget).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(farewellNotice.take()).toBe("Your account is deleted.");
  });

  it("treats a dead token on retry as already deleted", async () => {
    mockDelete.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError(401, "invalid_token", "Sign in again."));
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "pw");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    await flush();
    await flush();
    expect(mockForget).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("says nothing about venues or plans (they no longer exist)", async () => {
    await renderScreen();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/venue/i)).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText(/plans/i)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("shows a wrong password inline and stays signed in", async () => {
    mockDelete.mockRejectedValue(new ApiError(403, "wrong_password", "Password is incorrect"));
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "nope");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    await flush();
    expect(await screen.findByText("That password isn't right.")).toBeTruthy();
    expect(mockForget).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("does nothing if the confirmation is cancelled", async () => {
    alert.mockImplementationOnce(() => undefined);
    await renderScreen();
    await fireEvent.changeText(screen.getByTestId("delete-password"), "pw");
    await fireEvent.press(screen.getByLabelText("Delete account"));
    expect(mockDelete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});


===== src/features/people/__tests__/edit-profile.test.ts =====

import { goesPublic, goingPublicAlert, privacyNote, profileChanges, shouldOfferApproveAll } from "../edit-profile";

const priv = { displayName: "Sam", bio: "", isPrivate: true };

describe("going public", () => {
  it("is detected only when a private account is switched to public", () => {
    expect(goesPublic(priv, profileChanges(priv, { ...priv, isPrivate: false }))).toBe(true);
    expect(goesPublic(priv, profileChanges(priv, { ...priv, bio: "hi" }))).toBe(false);
    const pub = { ...priv, isPrivate: false };
    expect(goesPublic(pub, profileChanges(pub, { ...pub, isPrivate: true }))).toBe(false);
    expect(goesPublic(pub, profileChanges(pub, pub))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("the confirm alert lists exactly what changes: all stamps and every place, to anyone", () => {
    const a = goingPublicAlert({ stamps: 12, places: 5 });
    expect(a.title).toBe("Make your passport public?");
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/Anyone, signed in or not/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/all of your stamps \(photos, captions and songs\)/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/every place you've been/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/already stamped/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/12 stamps and 5 places/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/follow you without asking/);
    expect(a.message).toMatch(/switch back to private/);
    expect(a.confirm).toBe("Make public");
  });

  it("uses singular counts and works without counts", () => {
    expect(goingPublicAlert({ stamps: 1, places: 1 }).message).toMatch(/1 stamp and 1 place so far/);
    expect(goingPublicAlert().message).not.toMatch(/so far/);
    expect(goingPublicAlert({ stamps: 0, places: 0 }).message).not.toMatch(/so far/);
  });

  it("the toggle's note says what each state means and never mentions Anonymous", () => {
    expect(privacyNote(true)).toMatch(/Only people you approve see your stamps, places and counts/);
    expect(privacyNote(false)).toMatch(/Anyone can see your passport/);
    expect(privacyNote(true) + privacyNote(false)).not.toMatch(/Anonymous/);
  });

  it("offers Approve all only after going public with requests waiting", () => {
    expect(shouldOfferApproveAll({ isPrivate: false }, 3)).toBe(true);
    expect(shouldOfferApproveAll({ isPrivate: false }, 0)).toBe(false);
    expect(shouldOfferApproveAll({ isPrivate: true }, 3)).toBe(false);
  });
});

/** The passport screen and the follow button: what each viewer may see. */
import { QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";

let mockProfile: ProfileResponse;
const mockStamps = { data: { pages: [{ stamps: [], nextBefore: null }] }, isPending: false, hasNextPage: false, isFetchNextPageError: false, refetch: jest.fn() };

jest.mock("expo-router", () => ({ router: { push: jest.fn(), back: jest.fn() }, Stack: { Screen: () => null } }));

const relationship: ProfileResponse["relationship"] = { isSelf: false, follow: "none", followsYou: false, mutual: false };

describe("ProfileScreen", () => {

    expect(followButton("requested", "sam")).toMatchObject({ title: "Requested", variant: "secondary", label: "Cancel follow request to @sam" });
