===== src/components/ui/text-field.tsx =====

import { Children, forwardRef, Fragment, isValidElement, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { StyleSheet, TextInput, useWindowDimensions, View, type TextInputProps } from "react-native";

import { useAnnounce } from "@/lib/a11y";
import { colors, hairlineWidth, maxFontSizeMultiplier, radius, size, space, type as typeScale } from "@/theme";
import { Text } from "./text";

type FieldProps = TextInputProps & {
  label: string;
  /** Marks the row invalid; the message itself is shown in the group footer. */
  invalid?: boolean;
};

/** One row of an inset grouped form: label on the left, input on the right (stacked at large text sizes). */
export const Field = forwardRef<TextInput, FieldProps>(function Field({ label, invalid, style, accessibilityHint, ...rest }, ref) {
  const { fontScale } = useWindowDimensions();
  const stacked = fontScale > 1.3;
  return (
    <View style={[styles.row, stacked && styles.rowStacked]}>
      <Text
        variant="body"
        color={invalid ? "danger" : "textPrimary"}
        style={stacked ? undefined : styles.label}
        numberOfLines={stacked ? undefined : 1}
        accessibilityElementsHidden
        importantForAccessibility="no"
      >
        {label}
      </Text>
      <TextInput
        ref={ref}
        accessibilityLabel={invalid ? `${label}, needs attention` : label}
        accessibilityHint={accessibilityHint}
        placeholderTextColor={colors.textTertiary}
        selectionColor={colors.accent}
        keyboardAppearance="dark"
        maxFontSizeMultiplier={maxFontSizeMultiplier}
        {...rest}
        style={[styles.input, style]}
      />
    </View>
  );
});

type GroupProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
  /** Help text under the group. */
  footer?: string;
  /** Validation messages; replaces the footer while present and is announced to VoiceOver. */
  errors?: (string | null | undefined)[];
};

/** Inset grouped card of fields separated by hairlines, like iOS Settings. */
export function FieldGroup({ children, footer, errors }: GroupProps) {
  const messages = (errors ?? []).filter((e): e is string => !!e);
  useAnnounce(messages.join(" "));
  const rows = Children.toArray(children).filter(isValidElement);
  return (
    <View style={styles.groupWrap}>
      <View style={styles.group}>
        {rows.map((child, i) => (
          <Fragment key={child.key ?? i}>
            {i > 0 && <View style={styles.separator} />}
            {child}
          </Fragment>
        ))}
      </View>
      {messages.length > 0
        ? messages.map((m) => (
            <Text key={m} variant="footnote" color="danger" style={styles.footer}>
              {m}
            </Text>
          ))
        : !!footer && (
            <Text variant="footnote" color="textTertiary" style={styles.footer}>
              {footer}
            </Text>
          )}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  groupWrap: { gap: space.xs },
  group: { backgroundColor: colors.surface, borderRadius: radius.card, overflow: "hidden" },
  row: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center", minHeight: size.fieldHeight, paddingHorizontal: space.md, gap: space.sm },
  rowStacked: { flexDirection: "column", alignItems: "stretch", paddingVertical: space.sm, gap: space.xxs },
  label: { width: size.fieldLabel },
  input: { flex: 1, ...typeScale.body, color: colors.textPrimary, paddingVertical: space.sm },
  separator: { height: hairlineWidth, backgroundColor: colors.hairline, marginLeft: space.md },
  footer: { paddingHorizontal: space.md },
});


===== src/components/ui/text.tsx =====

import { Text as RNText, type TextProps as RNTextProps } from "react-native";

import { colors, maxFontSizeMultiplier, tabular as tabularStyle, type as typeScale, weight as weights, type ColorToken, type TypeVariant } from "@/theme";

export type TextProps = RNTextProps & {
  variant?: TypeVariant;
  color?: ColorToken;
  weight?: keyof typeof weights;
  /** Tabular figures for counts and money. */
  tabular?: boolean;
  align?: "left" | "center" | "right";
};

export function Text({ variant = "body", color = "textPrimary", weight, tabular, align, style, ...rest }: TextProps) {
  return (
    <RNText
      maxFontSizeMultiplier={maxFontSizeMultiplier}
      {...rest}
      style={[
        typeScale[variant],
        { color: colors[color] },
        weight && { fontWeight: weights[weight] },
        tabular && tabularStyle,
        align && { textAlign: align },
        style,
      ]}
    />
  );
}


===== src/components/ui/toast.tsx =====

import { useEffect, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { AccessibilityInfo, StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";
import Animated, { FadeInDown, FadeOutDown } from "react-native-reanimated";

import { colors, hairlineWidth, radius, size, space, timing } from "@/theme";
import { Icon } from "./icon";
import { Text } from "./text";

type Toast = { id: number; message: string; tone: "success" | "neutral" };

let current: Toast | null = null;
let seq = 0;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();
const emit = () => listeners.forEach((l) => l());

/** Short confirmations ("Vibe posted. Thanks."). Errors are shown inline, never as toasts. */
export const toast = {
  show(message: string, tone: Toast["tone"] = "success") {
    current = { id: ++seq, message, tone };
    // Announced here, once, however many hosts are mounted.
    AccessibilityInfo.announceForAccessibility(message);
    emit();
  },
  hide(id: number) {
    if (current?.id !== id) return;
    current = null;
    emit();
  },
  subscribe(l: () => void) {
    listeners.add(l);
    return () => listeners.delete(l);
  },
  get: () => current,
};

export const TOAST_MS = 2600;

/** Renders the current toast. Place one per native presentation (sheets sit above the root view). */
export function ToastHost({ bottom }: { bottom: number }) {
  const t = useSyncExternalStore(toast.subscribe, toast.get, toast.get);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!t) return;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => toast.hide(t.id), TOAST_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer);
  }, [t]);
  if (!t) return null;
  return (
    <View pointerEvents="none" style={[styles.wrap, { bottom }]} accessibilityElementsHidden importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants">
      <Animated.View key={t.id} entering={FadeInDown.duration(timing.standard)} exiting={FadeOutDown.duration(timing.fast)} style={styles.toast}>
        <Icon name={t.tone === "success" ? "checkmark.circle.fill" : "info.circle.fill"} color={t.tone === "success" ? "success" : "textSecondary"} size={size.iconSmall} />
        <Text variant="subhead" weight="semibold">
          {t.message}
        </Text>
      </Animated.View>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: { position: "absolute", left: 0, right: 0, alignItems: "center" },
  toast: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    gap: space.xs,
    paddingHorizontal: space.md,
    minHeight: size.hitTarget - space.xxs,
    borderRadius: radius.pill,
    backgroundColor: colors.elevated,
    borderWidth: hairlineWidth,
    borderColor: colors.hairline,
  },
});


===== src/components/ui/verified-badge.tsx =====

import { StyleSheet, View } from "react-native";

import { colors, size as sizes, verifiedDiamondScale } from "@/theme";
import { Icon } from "./icon";

/**
 * The verified badge: a small champagne diamond with a check. Shown next to a handle, only where the viewer may see
 * that handle (never on "Anonymous"). Spoken as "Verified" when it stands alone; rows that carry their own label
 * pass `accessible={false}` and say "verified" in that label instead.
 */
export function VerifiedBadge({ accessible = true }: { accessible?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <View
      style={styles.box}
      accessible={accessible}
      accessibilityRole={accessible ? "image" : undefined}
      accessibilityLabel={accessible ? "Verified" : undefined}
      importantForAccessibility={accessible ? "yes" : "no-hide-descendants"}
    >
      <View style={styles.diamond} />
      <Icon name="checkmark" color="onVerified" size={sizes.verifiedCheck} weight="black" />
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  box: { width: sizes.verifiedBadge, height: sizes.verifiedBadge, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  diamond: {
    ...StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
    backgroundColor: colors.verified,
    borderRadius: sizes.verifiedCorner,
    transform: [{ rotate: "45deg" }, { scale: verifiedDiamondScale }],
  },
});


===== eslint-rules/__tests__/rules.test.js =====

/**
 * Tests for the design-system lint rules, plus a scan of the real source tree so the rule
 * can't be silently switched off in eslint.config.js.
 */
const path = require("path");
const { execFileSync } = require("child_process");
const { RuleTester } = require("eslint");

const { rules } = require("..");

const tester = new RuleTester({
  languageOptions: { ecmaVersion: 2022, sourceType: "module", parserOptions: { ecmaFeatures: { jsx: true } } },
});

describe("local/no-raw-design-values", () => {
  tester.run("no-raw-design-values", rules["no-raw-design-values"], {
    valid: [
      "const s = { color: colors.accent, padding: space.md, borderRadius: radius.card };",
      "const s = { margin: 0, flex: 1, opacity: 0.5, zIndex: 2 };",
      "const label = 'Packed. 20 min line.';",
      "const tag = '#hashtag';",
      "<Icon size={size.icon} />",
      "const s = { borderRadius: size.marker / 2 };",
      "const label = { title: 'Red Room' };",
      "<Text color='textPrimary' />",
    ],
    invalid: [
      { code: "const s = { color: '#FFB224' };", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "const s = { backgroundColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)' };", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "const c = `hsl(10, 20%, 30%)`;", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "<View tint='#fff' />", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "const s = { padding: 17 };", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "const s = { marginLeft: -8 };", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "const s = { fontSize: 15, lineHeight: 20 };", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }, { messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "<Icon size={24} />", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "const s = { color: 'white' };", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "const s = { backgroundColor: 'Red' };", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "<Icon tintColor='black' />", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
      { code: "const s = { padding: 4 * 3 };", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "const s = { flexBasis: 120 };", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "<Pressable hitSlop={10} />", errors: [{ messageId: "number" }] },
      { code: "const c = PlatformColor('systemRed');", errors: [{ messageId: "color" }] },
    ],
  });
});

describe("local/no-emoji", () => {
  tester.run("no-emoji", rules["no-emoji"], {
    valid: ["const a = 'Packed · 20 min line';", "const b = '#1 spot';", "<Text>© OpenStreetMap contributors</Text>"],
    invalid: [
      { code: "const a = 'Packed 🔥';", errors: [{ messageId: "emoji" }] },
      { code: "<Text>Cheers 🍻</Text>", errors: [{ messageId: "emoji" }] },
    ],
  });
});

describe("the source tree", () => {
  it("has no raw colors or emoji outside src/theme, and the rules are enabled", () => {
    const out = execFileSync(process.execPath, [path.join(__dirname, "../scan.js")], { encoding: "utf8", timeout: 120000 });
    const { rules: enabled, hits, files } = JSON.parse(out);
    expect(enabled).toEqual({ raw: [2], emoji: [2] });
    expect(files).toBeGreaterThan(10);
    expect(hits).toEqual([]);
  });
});


===== eslint-rules/index.js =====

/**
 * Local lint rules that enforce the design system (see src/theme/).
 *
 * - no-raw-design-values: raw colors (hex, rgb[a], hsl[a]) anywhere outside src/theme/, and raw
 *   numbers for spacing, radius, size or type properties in style objects.
 * - no-emoji: emoji in UI strings. SF Symbols only.
 */
const COLOR_RE = /#(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{3,4}|[0-9a-fA-F]{6}|[0-9a-fA-F]{8})\b|\b(?:rgba?|hsla?|hwb|lab|lch|oklab|oklch|color)\s*\(/;

// CSS named colors (React Native accepts all of them). Matched only when the whole string is a color name.
const NAMED_COLORS = new Set(
  (
    "aliceblue antiquewhite aqua aquamarine azure beige bisque black blanchedalmond blue blueviolet brown burlywood " +
    "cadetblue chartreuse chocolate coral cornflowerblue cornsilk crimson cyan darkblue darkcyan darkgoldenrod darkgray " +
    "darkgreen darkgrey darkkhaki darkmagenta darkolivegreen darkorange darkorchid darkred darksalmon darkseagreen " +
    "darkslateblue darkslategray darkslategrey darkturquoise darkviolet deeppink deepskyblue dimgray dimgrey dodgerblue " +
    "firebrick floralwhite forestgreen fuchsia gainsboro ghostwhite gold goldenrod gray green greenyellow grey honeydew " +
    "hotpink indianred indigo ivory khaki lavender lavenderblush lawngreen lemonchiffon lightblue lightcoral lightcyan " +
    "lightgoldenrodyellow lightgray lightgreen lightgrey lightpink lightsalmon lightseagreen lightskyblue lightslategray " +
    "lightslategrey lightsteelblue lightyellow lime limegreen linen magenta maroon mediumaquamarine mediumblue mediumorchid " +
    "mediumpurple mediumseagreen mediumslateblue mediumspringgreen mediumturquoise mediumvioletred midnightblue mintcream " +
    "mistyrose moccasin navajowhite navy oldlace olive olivedrab orange orangered orchid palegoldenrod palegreen " +
    "paleturquoise palevioletred papayawhip peachpuff peru pink plum powderblue purple rebeccapurple red rosybrown " +
    "royalblue saddlebrown salmon sandybrown seagreen seashell sienna silver skyblue slateblue slategray slategrey snow " +
    "springgreen steelblue tan teal thistle tomato turquoise violet wheat white whitesmoke yellow yellowgreen transparent"
  ).split(" "),
);

// Props and style keys whose value is a color.
const COLOR_KEYS = /(?:^|[a-z])(?:[cC]olor|tint|Tint)$|^(?:backgroundColor|color|tintColor|shadowColor|borderColor|placeholderTextColor|selectionColor|underlayColor|tint)$/;

const STYLE_KEYS = new Set([
  "padding", "paddingTop", "paddingBottom", "paddingLeft", "paddingRight", "paddingHorizontal", "paddingVertical", "paddingStart", "paddingEnd",
  "margin", "marginTop", "marginBottom", "marginLeft", "marginRight", "marginHorizontal", "marginVertical", "marginStart", "marginEnd",
  "gap", "rowGap", "columnGap",
  "top", "bottom", "left", "right",
  "width", "height", "minWidth", "minHeight", "maxWidth", "maxHeight",
  "borderRadius", "borderTopLeftRadius", "borderTopRightRadius", "borderBottomLeftRadius", "borderBottomRightRadius",
  "borderWidth", "borderTopWidth", "borderBottomWidth", "borderLeftWidth", "borderRightWidth",
  "fontSize", "lineHeight", "letterSpacing", "shadowRadius", "flexBasis",
]);

// Numeric JSX props that are sizes.
const SIZE_PROPS = new Set(["size", "hitSlop", "strokeWidth", "iconSize"]);

function keyName(node) {
  if (!node) return null;
  if (node.type === "Identifier") return node.name;
  if (node.type === "Literal" && typeof node.value === "string") return node.value;
  return null;
}

function numericLiteral(node) {
  if (node.type === "Literal" && typeof node.value === "number") return node.value;
  if (node.type === "UnaryExpression" && node.operator === "-" && node.argument.type === "Literal" && typeof node.argument.value === "number") {
    return -node.argument.value;
  }
  // Pure arithmetic on literals (`4 * 3`) is a raw value in disguise. Token math (`size.marker / 2`) is fine.
  if (node.type === "BinaryExpression") {
    const l = numericLiteral(node.left);
    const r = numericLiteral(node.right);
    if (l !== null && r !== null) return l;
  }
  return null;
}

function stringValue(node) {
  if (!node) return null;
  if (node.type === "Literal" && typeof node.value === "string") return node.value;
  if (node.type === "TemplateLiteral" && node.expressions.length === 0) return node.quasis[0].value.cooked;
  return null;
}

const noRawDesignValues = {
  meta: {
    type: "problem",
    docs: { description: "Colors, spacing, radius, size and type values must come from src/theme." },
    messages: {
      color: "Raw color '{{value}}'. Use a token from src/theme/colors.ts.",
      number: "Raw {{key}}: {{value}}. Use a token from src/theme (space, radius, size, type).",
    },
    schema: [],
  },
  create(context) {
    const checkString = (node, value) => {
      if (typeof value === "string" && COLOR_RE.test(value)) {
        context.report({ node, messageId: "color", data: { value: value.length > 40 ? `${value.slice(0, 40)}…` : value } });
      }
    };
    return {
      Literal(node) {
        checkString(node, node.value);
      },
      TemplateElement(node) {
        checkString(node, node.value.cooked);
      },
      Property(node) {
        const key = keyName(node.key);
        if (!key) return;
        const str = stringValue(node.value);
        if (str !== null && COLOR_KEYS.test(key) && NAMED_COLORS.has(str.trim().toLowerCase())) {
          context.report({ node: node.value, messageId: "color", data: { value: str } });
        }
        if (!STYLE_KEYS.has(key)) return;
        const n = numericLiteral(node.value);
        // 0 is not a design value; everything else must be a token.
        if (n !== null && n !== 0) context.report({ node: node.value, messageId: "number", data: { key, value: String(n) } });
      },
      JSXAttribute(node) {
        const key = node.name && node.name.name;
        if (typeof key !== "string") return;
        const expr = node.value && node.value.type === "JSXExpressionContainer" ? node.value.expression : node.value;
        const str = stringValue(expr);
        if (str !== null && COLOR_KEYS.test(key) && NAMED_COLORS.has(str.trim().toLowerCase())) {
          context.report({ node: expr, messageId: "color", data: { value: str } });
        }
        if (!STYLE_KEYS.has(key) && !SIZE_PROPS.has(key)) return;
        const n = expr ? numericLiteral(expr) : null;
        if (n !== null && n !== 0) context.report({ node: expr, messageId: "number", data: { key, value: String(n) } });
      },
      CallExpression(node) {
        // PlatformColor("systemRed") bypasses the palette.
        if (node.callee.type === "Identifier" && (node.callee.name === "PlatformColor" || node.callee.name === "DynamicColorIOS")) {
          context.report({ node, messageId: "color", data: { value: node.callee.name } });
        }
      },
    };
  },
};

// Extended_Pictographic covers emoji; exclude the ASCII-range symbols (#, *, digits) it also matches.
const EMOJI_RE = /(?![#*0-9©®])\p{Extended_Pictographic}/u;

const noEmoji = {
  meta: {
    type: "problem",
    docs: { description: "No emoji in the UI. Use SF Symbols via the Icon component." },
    messages: { emoji: "Emoji in UI text. Use an SF Symbol (components/ui/icon) instead." },
    schema: [],
  },
  create(context) {
    const check = (node, value) => {
      if (typeof value === "string" && EMOJI_RE.test(value)) context.report({ node, messageId: "emoji" });
    };
    return {
      Literal: (node) => check(node, node.value),
      TemplateElement: (node) => check(node, node.value.cooked),
      JSXText: (node) => check(node, node.value),
    };
  },
};

module.exports = { rules: { "no-raw-design-values": noRawDesignValues, "no-emoji": noEmoji } };


===== eslint-rules/scan.js =====

/** Prints the design-rule config and violations for src/ as JSON. Run by the Jest test in a child process. */
const path = require("path");
const { ESLint } = require("eslint");

(async () => {
  const root = path.resolve(__dirname, "..");
  // allowInlineConfig: false so `eslint-disable` comments can't hide a violation from this check.
  const eslint = new ESLint({ cwd: root, allowInlineConfig: false });
  const config = await eslint.calculateConfigForFile(path.join(root, "src/app/_layout.tsx"));
  const results = await eslint.lintFiles(["src/**/*.{ts,tsx}"]);
  const hits = results.flatMap((r) =>
    r.messages
      .filter((m) => m.ruleId === "local/no-raw-design-values" || m.ruleId === "local/no-emoji")
      .map((m) => `${path.relative(root, r.filePath)}:${m.line} ${m.message}`),
  );
  process.stdout.write(
    JSON.stringify({ rules: { raw: config.rules["local/no-raw-design-values"], emoji: config.rules["local/no-emoji"] }, hits, files: results.length }),
  );
})().catch((e) => {
  process.stderr.write(String(e && e.stack));
  process.exit(1);
});
